## Fauna of Shuigari

# Fauna of Shuigari

Shuigari's wildlife reflects the archipelago's balance between spirit and sword. Creatures often carry cultural meaning, appear in omens, or lend their traits to local craftsmanship. Use the table below to bring the natural world to the forefront of encounters and downtime scenes.

| Creature | Type | Description | Habitat | Cultural Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **Ashwing Raven** | Bird | Charcoal raven with ashen wingtips and a keen mimicry call. | [Takashima Prefecture](Takashima%20Prefecture.md) highlands | Considered heralds of change; feathers anchor divination charms. |
| **Riverguard Turtle** | Reptile | Massive freshwater turtle cloaked in moss and algae. | Minashima river valleys | Villagers carve shells into ceremonial shields representing patience. |
| **Skyflash Hawk** | Bird of prey | Sleek hawk with lightning-streaked plumage and uncanny speed. | Kisato plateau and trade routes | Employed as message hawks by scholars and magistrates. |
| **Flamehorn Yak** | Mammal | Mountain yak whose horns glow faintly at night. | Takashima alpine passes | Horn shavings fuel rituals for safe travel; beasts haul forge supplies. |
| **Crimson Fang Tiger** | Mammal | Rare tiger with crimson striping and elongated sabre fangs. | Furenhoku tundra forests | Sightings are omens of either great fortune or impending disaster. |
| **Sea Blossom Dolphin** | Aquatic mammal | Playful dolphin patterned like blooming petals. | Sea of Mirrors | Fisherfolk credit pods with guiding ships through treacherous shoals. |
| **Moonlit Koi** | Fish | Silver-scaled koi that glow under moonlight. | Temple ponds across Hikosan | Released during moon festivals to honor [Tsukuyomi](The%20worship%20of%20Tsukuyomi%20in%20Shuigari.md). |
| **Amberback Boar** | Mammal | Hefty boar with amber carapace-like bristles. | Frostwood outskirts | Hunts are dedicated to local kami; tusks carved into protective talismans. |

Sprinkle these beasts into travel montages, merchandise lists, or lore dumps to reinforce Shuigari's blend of mysticism and pragmatism.

## Food and Drink of Shuigari

# Food and Drink of Shuigari

Shuigari's cuisine mirrors its geography: mountain villages favor hearty fare that wards off alpine winds, coastal prefectures celebrate the day's catch, and the capital indulges in refined dishes served alongside fragrant teas. Use the following spreads to ground tavern scenes, festival banquets, or downtime visits to the market.

## Signature Dishes

| Dish | Type | Description | Region | Cultural Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **Rice Bowl (Gohan)** | Staple | Steamed rice served with pickled greens or miso broth. | Nationwide | Forms the foundation of every meal regardless of class. |
| **Takashima Iron Stew** | Hearty stew | Root vegetables, mountain herbs, and smoked meat simmered overnight. | [Takashima Prefecture](Takashima%20Prefecture.md) | Favored by miners and smiths; often shared after long forge shifts. |
| **Yuzu-Glazed Fish** | Seafood | Grilled river fish lacquered with sweet-tart yuzu glaze. | [Harado Prefecture](Harado%20Prefecture.md) | Served during harbor festivals and family gatherings among sailors. |
| **Forest Mushroom Hotpot** | Communal stew | Wild mushrooms, bamboo shoots, and tofu simmered in savory broth. | [Shugane](Shugane.md) & surrounding forests | Hunters bring ingredients as tribute to local kami before sharing the meal. |
| **Mountain Yak Dumplings** | Snack | Steamed dumplings filled with spiced yak and mountain leeks. | [Takashima Prefecture](Takashima%20Prefecture.md) | Packed by travelers crossing the Frostwood passes; sold in roadside stalls. |
| **Celestial Sunrise Porridge** | Breakfast | Millet porridge with ginger, honey, and sun-dried peaches. | Hikosan Prefecture | Served to visiting dignitaries before morning petitions at court. |
| **Crimson Berry Skewers** | Street food | Skewered berries brushed with rice syrup and toasted sesame. | Coastal markets | Sold at lantern festivals; rumored to grant good fortune in romance. |
| **Hunter's Skewer** | Trail ration | Charred strips of boar or stag with coarse salt and pepper. | Takashima & Frostwood Outpost | Standard ration for rangers and patrols; often paired with hot sake. |

| Drink | Type | Description | Region | Cultural Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **Green Tea (Ryokucha)** | Hot beverage | Fresh-steeped leaves with grassy aroma. | Nationwide | Poured for guests as a sign of respect and welcome. |
| **Rice Wine (Sake)** | Alcoholic | Fermented rice wine with varying sweetness. | Nationwide | Served warm in winter, chilled in summer; central to shrine festivals. |
| **Plum Wine (Umeshu)** | Alcoholic | Sweet wine made by steeping ume fruit in shochu. | [Shugane](Shugane.md) orchards | Toasted during moon-viewing parties and betrothal ceremonies. |
| **Crimson Berry Wine** | Alcoholic | Tart, ruby wine crafted from coastal berries. | [Harado Prefecture](Harado%20Prefecture.md) | Paired with seafood feasts and celebratory returns from long voyages. |
| **Yak Butter Tea** | Hot beverage | Black tea whisked with yak butter and roasted barley flour. | Takashima Highlands | Keeps patrols warm on night watches; doubles as a hardy breakfast. |
| **Moonpetal Infusion** | Herbal tisane | Brewed moonvine petals with lavender and silvergrass. | Temple of the Three Moons | Shared during vigils to invite dreams and divinations from Tsukuyomi. |
| **Sea Breeze Iced Tea** | Cold beverage | Coastal herbs, dried kelp, and citrus peel shaken over ice. | Harado docks | Popular with dockworkers seeking a caffeine-free refresher. |

Use these tables when stocking taverns, rewarding downtime, or underscoring regional identity. Swapping a stew or tea for one of these specialties immediately signals where the characters are--and whose hospitality they've earned.

## Frostwood Outpost

# Frostwood Outpost

Perched on Nagano's northern edge, the Frostwood Outpost marks the last palisade before the haunted woods. Militia patrols, traveling merchants, and nascent adventurers all cycle through its gates, making it a natural staging ground for missions tied to *The Last Whisper of Steel*.

## Layout
- **Watchtower:** Wooden tower with signal lanterns and a clear view of the Frostwood treeline.
- **Barracks:** Bunks for up to twenty militia members, plus an armory stocked with polearms, bows, and emergency Soulsteel shards.
- **Palisade & Gatehouse:** Sharpened log walls and a permanently manned gate.
- **Supply Depot:** Stores dry rations, healing kits, and cold-weather gear; doubles as a rescue cache.
- **Shrine of Tsuchi-no-Megumi:** Militia offer prayers to the Earth Kami before each patrol; shrine doubles as a ritual site when spiritual imbalance is detected.

## Command Staff
- **Captain Shiro Tanabe (half-orc):** Tactical, even-tempered, and fiercely protective of Nagano. Values clear intel and decisive action.
- **Lieutenant Akari Fujita (elf):** Lead scout with encyclopedic knowledge of Frostwood trails; brings dry humor and detailed field reports.
- **Militia Rotations:** Twelve to twenty guards on staggered shifts covering sentry duty, supply management, and quick-response patrols.

## Core Duties
- **Defensive Screen:** Patrols the Nagano road and keeps watch for yokai, bandits, or Obsidian Circle agents testing the boundary.
- **Traveler Aid:** Issues warnings, weather reports, and temporary shelter to caravans or pilgrim groups.
- **Intelligence Hub:** Consolidates sightings of yokai movement, shrine desecrations, and unusual celestial events for the mayor and temple leadership.
- **Emergency Response:** Acts as launch point for rescue missions when villagers or scouts vanish within the Frostwood.

## Ongoing Problems (Adventure Hooks)
- **Missing Scouts:** Two rangers vanished after reporting strange chittering near a sealed shrine. Captain Tanabe offers a sizable reward for confirmation of their fate.
- **Jiro the Thorn's Bandits:** A clever highwayman uses the forest canopy for ambushes. Kana Yoshiro seeks allies to smoke him out.
- **Spiritual Disruption:** The shrine's wards flicker during Tsukuyomi's brightest nights. Reiko Matsuda suspects corruption spreading beneath the roots.
- **Raven Yokai Sign:** A giant raven yokai circled the outpost during Hideo's funeral. Interpreting its omen could change how Nagano prepares for the coming conflict.

Use the outpost as a repeatable stopover: characters can resupply, gather rumors, and feel the weight of duty as the Frostwood presses ever closer.

## Harado Prefecture

# Harado Prefecture

To the west lies Harado Prefecture, a realm of thriving trade and cultural diversity. Governed from the bustling trade city of Harado, the prefecture's daimyo oversees a region where wealth and opportunity are the true measures of power. The city of Harado itself is a place of vibrant markets and lavish estates, where merchants and nobles alike gather to exchange goods and forge alliances. The daimyo of Harado is known as a shrewd and ambitious ruler, always seeking to expand his influence both within the prefecture and across the archipelago.

The prefecture's settlements are as varied as its landscape. Takeshi, a small fishing village on the northern coast, is famous for its skillful fishermen who navigate the dangerous waters to harvest the rich bounty of the sea. The riverside village of Kiragawa is known for its pristine waters, where pilgrims come to bathe and cleanse their spirits, and its skilled boatmen who guide travelers along the prefecture's waterways. Komatsu and Yorimatsu, both prosperous farming villages, produce much of the prefecture's food supply, their fields yielding bountiful harvests of rice and vegetables. Tsubasa, a remote mountain town, is known for its serene hot springs and its reputation as a place of healing, where those seeking refuge from the turmoil of court life come to restore their spirits.

The daimyo of Harado, though nominally loyal to the Shogunate, has long harbored ambitions of greater power. His control over the island's western trade routes grants him significant leverage, and his web of alliances with merchant guilds and lesser clans makes him a formidable force in [Shugane](Shugane.md)'s political landscape.

## Haruto the Blacksmith

# Haruto the Blacksmith

**Quote:** "He repaired my father's broken sword."

A local smith inspired by [Hideo](Master%20Hideo%20Nakata.md)'s generosity, Haruto remembers how Hideo restored a family heirloom free of charge when they couldn't afford the repair.

## Hideos-Steel-Tablet-Last-Will

# Master Hideo's Last Will and Testament - The Steel Tablet

**Item:** Hideo's Steel Tablet of Final Instructions  
**Bearer:** Kazuki Takemura (The Twice-Cursed Samurai)  
**Significance:** Last will, mission directive, and list of trusted allies  
**Revelation:** The party is named on it - Hideo knew about them before he died

---

## THE TABLET - PHYSICAL DESCRIPTION

### Appearance

**Material:** 
- Folded steel (tamahagane) - same material used for soulsteel blades
- Six layers forged together, creating subtle wave pattern
- 8 inches tall, 5 inches wide, half-inch thick
- Surprisingly heavy for its size (3 pounds)

**Surface:**
- Polished to mirror finish on both sides
- Hideo's maker's mark stamped into top center (mountain and anvil)
- Text inscribed with acid etching - permanent, cannot be altered
- Characters filled with silver inlay for contrast
- Edges beveled, smooth - no sharp corners

**Condition:**
- Immaculate despite 3 years of carrying
- Kazuki cleans it daily (ritual, remembrance)
- No rust, no tarnish - soulsteel never degrades
- Warm to the touch when held (resonates with living steel)

**Carrying:**
- Wrapped in silk cloth (indigo with Hideo's mark)
- Secured to Kazuki's obi with leather cord
- He touches it frequently (unconscious gesture, comfort)
- Never lets it out of his sight

---

## THE INSCRIPTION - FRONT SIDE

### Master Hideo's Final Words (Read Aloud)

> The front of the tablet bears Hideo's maker's mark at the top - a mountain with an anvil at its base, struck through with a single vertical line. Below it, in elegant characters etched deep and filled with silver:
>
> **"The Last Instructions of Master Hideo Nakata, Smith of the Mountain, Guardian of the First Forge"**
>
> *Written on the Night of the Broken Moon, in my final hours of clarity*
>
> *To whoever finds this: My time ends not by age but by necessity. The Obsidian Circle seeks what I know, and I will not let my knowledge serve their unmaking. I have asked Kazuki Takemura to grant me the mercy of a swift end, and he has agreed. Judge him not - he acts on my plea, not his desire.*
>
> *What lies beneath Takashima must remain sealed. The Circle seeks the First Chain - the binding that holds Tsurogami in its prison. They will use the boy heir's blood as the key. They will corrupt what should remain pure. They will unmake what should remain whole.*
>
> *I have seen this in steel and flame. The forge never lies.*
>
> *To those who would stand against this darkness, I leave my final work: a list of names. These souls will arrive when needed. Trust them. Guide them. Give them what they need to succeed where I have failed.*
>
> *The furnace remembers the steel it forged. Let them remember me.*
>
> **- Hideo Nakata, Smith**
> **Year 2302, Autumn Moon**

---

## THE INSCRIPTION - BACK SIDE

### The Mission and The Names

> Kazuki turns the tablet over. The back is divided into two sections:

### SECTION 1: TAKEMURA'S MISSION (Top Third)

> **"To Kazuki Takemura, the Twice-Cursed, who carries my final burden:"**
>
> *Guard what they seek until those who can truly protect it arrive. You cannot enter the citadel, but you can guide those who can. You cannot break your curses, but you can prevent others from suffering as you have. You cannot save everyone, but you can save this city.*
>
> *When the names below arrive in Takashima, find them. Test them. If they are worthy, employ them. If they are true, trust them with what I showed you.*
>
> *The vault beneath the citadel holds the First Chain. Only those with fire in their hearts and steel in their souls can protect it. Do not face this alone. You have suffered alone too long.*
>
> *This is my final request, and my final gift: permission to trust again.*

### SECTION 2: THE LIST OF NAMES (Bottom Two-Thirds)

> **"Those Who Will Come - The Fire and the Steel:"**
>
> *(Each name is inscribed with a single identifying phrase)*

---

## THE REVELATION SCENE

### Kazuki's Dramatic Reveal (After his story, before the proposal)

**Modified Scene Flow:**

After Kazuki explains his two curses and Hideo's death, add this moment:

> Kazuki's hand moves to his obi. From within the folds of cloth, he produces something wrapped in indigo silk bearing Hideo's mark.
>
> "Before Master Hideo asked me to end his life, he gave me this. His final work. Not a blade - those days were past him. This."
>
> He unwraps the silk carefully, almost reverently. Lamplight catches on polished steel.
>
> A tablet. Perfectly rectangular. Mirror-bright. Inscribed with silver-filled characters that seem to glow in the tea house's warm light.
>
> "His last will and testament. Written the night he died. The night I..." (His voice catches) "...honored his final request."
>
> He places it on the table between you. You see Hideo's maker's mark. You see the title. You see the date.
>
> Three years ago. The night of Master Hideo's death.
>
> "Read it," Kazuki says quietly. "The front first. Then the back. And then... then you'll understand why I came to find you specifically."

**Allow Players to Read/Study:**

Let them actually read the inscriptions above (or paraphrase/summarize as you read aloud). The key moment is when they turn to the back and see the list of names.

### The Impact Moment (Read Aloud)

> You turn the tablet over. At the top: Kazuki's mission, Hideo's final instructions to the Twice-Cursed.
>
> And below that...
>
> Names.
>
> Twenty names. Twenty souls Hideo deemed critical to protecting what lies beneath Takashima.
>
> And at the very top, listed first, designated "The Six Flames"...
>
> Your names.
>
> All of you.
>
> Not just your names - descriptions. Specific details. Things Hideo should not have known. Could not have known.
>
> **Fox, the Masked Assassin - "Bearer of hidden face, seeker of lost master, carrier of implanted memories"**
>
> He knew about Kenshin. About the false memories. About Fox's search.
>
> **Shigeo, the Braggart Brawler - "Marked by Greed's Embrace, louder than thunder, stronger than doubt"**  
>
> He knew about the curse. The one that drains five hit points from Shigeo's maximum. The burden you carry.
>
> **Aoshi, the Last Waban - "Final guardian of fallen order, seer of visions, bearer of oni blood"**
>
> He knew the Oniwaban had fallen. He knew Aoshi was the last. He knew about Shikigo, Oni Babamon.
>
> **Thornbringer, the Druid - "Seeker of the Well, speaker to growth, walker between wild and steel"**
>
> He knew about your quest. The Well of Life. The thing you've been searching for.
>
> **Kanji, the Haunted Acrobat - "Hero of the chasm, resolver of guilt, investigator of shadows"**
>
> The chasm crossing. Akemi Takahashi. The guilt you carry, now resolved. He knew.
>
> **Yasei, Servant of Hikari - "Voice of the silenced kami, hope of the Deep Village, light in darkness"**
>
> Hikari. The portal. The Deep Village's hope. The silence of the kami. All of it.
>
> Kazuki watches you process this. His scarred face is unreadable, but his voice is soft:
>
> "Three years ago, Master Hideo knew you were coming. He knew your names. Your burdens. Your purposes. He inscribed them in steel - the one material that cannot lie - and told me to wait. To find you when you arrived in Takashima. To test you. And if you proved worthy..."
>
> He touches the tablet.
>
> "...to give you everything. His knowledge. His allies. His final mission."
>
> (Pause, let it sink in)
>
> "How did he know? I don't know. The forge shows him things, he said. Steel remembers what will be forged from it. He saw you in the flames and the folding metal. He saw you coming."
>
> (Meets each of your eyes in turn)
>
> "And he trusted you to finish what he could not. To protect what he died protecting. To stand where he fell."

---

## PLAYER REACTIONS & RESPONSES

### Common Questions & Kazuki's Answers

**"How did Hideo know about us?"**
> "He never explained fully. Said the forge showed him visions. Said steel speaks to those who listen deeply enough. Said he saw 'six flames approaching a mountain of iron.' Whether it was prophecy, divine insight, or simply the wisdom of a dying master... I don't know. But the tablet doesn't lie. Steel cannot lie."

**"Why are WE on this list?"**
> "You tell me. You're the ones who came to Takashima despite the warnings. You're the ones who rescued Kazuo when others would have left him. You're the ones sitting in a cursed tea house after meeting a twice-cursed ronin, still asking questions instead of running. Perhaps Master Hideo saw in you what I'm beginning to see: people who don't abandon what's right just because it's hard."

**"Who are the other names?"**
> "Allies. Some you've already met." (Gestures to Kadei, Minari, Kazuo on list) "Others you'll need to find. Some..." (Touches Lady Hana and Tenka's names) "...are warnings. And some..." (Touches Master Chen and Yuki) "...are ghosts who deserve justice."

**"Can we see the tablet / hold it?"**
> "Yes." (He offers it) "Master Hideo meant for you to see it. To know he trusted you before you even knew he existed. To understand this isn't random chance - you were called here. By a dead smith's final vision. By steel and flame. By whatever force moves heroes to where they're needed most."

**"Why didn't you show us this immediately?"**
> "Would you have believed me? A cursed ronin appears with a steel tablet claiming a dead master prophesied your arrival three years ago? You would have thought me mad. No. First, I needed to tell you my story. Show you my burden. Give you context. Now... now you understand what this means. What HE meant for you to do."

---

## MECHANICAL & NARRATIVE IMPLICATIONS

### What This Changes

**Trust Level:**
- This isn't just a job offer from a desperate ronin
- This is a DESTINY given by Master Hideo himself
- The party was specifically chosen, foreseen, trusted
- Makes the mission deeply personal

**Connections:**
- Every name on the list is a potential ally
- Some they've already met (validation of their path)
- Some they'll need to find (quest hooks)
- Some are warnings (creates tension)

**Player Agency:**
- Despite prophecy, they still choose
- Hideo trusted them to make the right choice
- But they're not forced - free will matters
- Refusal is still possible (but harder emotionally)

**Fox's Connection:**
- Master Hideo knew about the implanted memories
- Mentions "lost master" - validates Fox's grief
- Makes Hideo's death even more meaningful
- Fox was NAMED by the smith they mourn

**Campaign Weight:**
- Raises stakes significantly
- Not just helping a ronin - fulfilling a dead master's vision
- Every other name becomes important
- The list provides structure for Arc 2

---

## THE TABLET AS QUEST TRACKER

### How to Use This in Play

**Visual Aid:**
- Print or display the list for players
- Let them cross off names as they meet people
- Add notes about each person's status
- Track who they can trust vs. who's compromised

**Investigation Hook:**
- "Why did Hideo warn against Lady Hana specifically?"
- "Who is Tenka and why is he dangerous?"
- "What happened to Master Chen and Yuki?"

**Ally Recruitment:**
- Each name is a potential quest to recruit
- Some are easy (Kaoru, Mama Tora)
- Some require proving themselves (General Kenta)
- Some are complicated (High Priestess Amaya)

**Tragedy Tracker:**
- Names crossed out if allies fall
- Names moved to "Lost" if they're corrupted
- Each loss hurts more because Hideo CHOSE them

---

## INTEGRATION WITH EXISTING SCENES

### Tea House Scene Addition

**Insert this revelation:**
- AFTER: Kazuki's story of his curses and killing Hideo
- BEFORE: His proposal to infiltrate the Upper Citadel
- TIMING: 5-7 minutes for full revelation and player processing

**New Scene Flow:**
1. Kazuki tells his story (curses, Hideo's death)
2. **[NEW]** He reveals the steel tablet
3. **[NEW]** Players read and realize they're named on it
4. **[NEW]** Process the implications (destiny, trust, purpose)
5. Kazuki makes his proposal (now with greater weight)
6. Combat with Shadeblade Cultists (unchanged)
7. The choice to accept or decline (now much harder to decline)

### Mama Tora's Name

When players see Mama Tora's name on the list:
> Mama Tora, who has been listening quietly, freezes when you read her name aloud.
>
> "Hideo... put me on that?" Her voice is rough. "Daughter of iron, widow of courage... keeper of cursed street."
>
> She's silent for a long moment. Then:
>
> "Kuro died three years ago. Same time Hideo did. Same time this was written. He knew. Somehow he knew I'd end up here, alone, holding this place together with spite and sake."
>
> She refills everyone's cups with shaking hands.
>
> "If Hideo trusted you enough to put you on that tablet... then I trust you too. The dead don't lie. Neither does steel."

### Kaoru's Name

When Kaoru arrives later and sees her name on the list:
> Kaoru goes very still when she sees her name.
>
> "Third Bloom of hidden petals, weaver of stories, seeker of truth." She reads it twice. "Master Hideo knew about the Society? More than that - he trusted us enough to..."
>
> She touches the inscription reverently.
>
> "We've been investigating the Circle for three years. Same time this was written. As if he knew we'd need to be organized, ready, watching. As if our whole purpose was to be here for this moment."
>
> She looks at the party with new respect.
>
> "If you're on this list... if HE saw you coming... then perhaps we actually have a chance."

---

## OPTIONAL: THE PROPHECY DETAIL

### If You Want to Add More Mystery

**Hideo's Vision (Kazuki can share this if asked):**

> "The night he wrote the tablet, Master Hideo told me what he saw in the forge flames:
>
> 'Six flames approaching a mountain of iron. Each flame burns with different color - silver, gold, violet, green, crimson, white. They arrive when the mountain begins to crack from within. They arrive when the boy speaks with stolen voice. They arrive when the cursed street weeps. They arrive when the twice-cursed seeks redemption.'
>
> (Pause)
>
> 'The flames can stop the cracking. Or they can be extinguished trying. The steel hasn't decided yet which future to forge. But I will give them every tool. Every name. Every secret. And I will trust that flames, given fuel, choose to burn bright rather than die out.'
>
> That's what he said. Word for word. I've repeated it every night for three years, waiting for the six flames to arrive."
>
> (Looks at each party member)
>
> "And here you are."

---

## UPDATED SESSION 019 REWARDS

### If Party Accepts After Seeing the Tablet

**In Addition to Original Rewards:**
- **Master Hideo's Trust:** Each party member gains Inspiration
- **Steel Testament:** The tablet can be consulted for ally information
- **Prophecy Weight:** Advantage on first Persuasion check with any named ally on the list
- **Destiny's Burden:** Party knows they were chosen - this weighs on decisions

---

## GM NOTES: HANDLING THE REVEAL

### Pacing Tips

1. **Build Up Slowly:** Don't rush the revelation
2. **Let Players Process:** Give them time to react to each detail
3. **Individual Moments:** Address each PC's entry separately
4. **Emotional Weight:** This should feel HUGE
5. **Kazuki's Reverence:** He treats the tablet as holy relic

### Player Agency Reminder

Despite prophecy:
- They can still refuse
- Destiny isn't compulsion
- Choice matters MORE because they were chosen
- Refusal would be valid (but narratively costly)

### If Party is Skeptical

> "I understand doubt. I doubted too. But answer me this: How did a man three years dead know your names? Your burdens? Your specific struggles? How did he know you'd arrive in Takashima at this exact time? Coincidence? Perhaps. But steel doesn't forge coincidences. Steel forges truth."

---

## CONCLUSION

**The steel tablet transforms the tea house scene from:**
- Simple job offer ? Divine calling
- Random employment ? Prophesied destiny  
- Helping a ronin ? Fulfilling a master's vision
- Individual choice ? Part of larger pattern

**But most importantly:**
- It honors Master Hideo's sacrifice
- It validates the party's journey to Takashima
- It connects them to something greater
- It gives them a network of named allies
- It makes the mission PERSONAL

*The furnace remembers the steel it forged. Let them remember him.*

---

*Use this tablet to deepen investment and provide structure. Every name is a thread. Every thread leads somewhere. And at the center: six flames, approaching a mountain of iron, carrying the hope of a dead master's final vision.*

## Hikosan Prefecture

# Hikosan Prefecture

## Overview

Hikosan Prefecture sits at the heart of [Shugane](Shugane.md) and serves as the political and spiritual core of the archipelago. The **Shogunate** governs from the imperial palace within the capital city of **Hikosan**, drawing courtiers, diplomats, and warrior envoys from every prefecture. Daily life in the province is defined by ceremony and intrigue; audiences with the Shogun, petitions to the court, and rival delegations all vie for influence under the watchful eyes of shrine attendants and palace guards.  

The prefecture balances bustling urban streets with carefully maintained sacred spaces. Pilgrims move alongside merchants hauling tribute, while itinerant monks debate policy with samurai envoys in teahouses overlooking the palace gardens. Stability is maintained through the Shogun's presence, yet that same concentration of power raises the stakes for every political decision.

## Key Settlements

- **Hikosan (Capital):** Seat of the Shogunate, home to the imperial palace, the Council of Blades, and the Great Archive of Edicts. The city's concentric districts separate noble estates from artisan quarters and crowded merchant wards.
- **Takayama:** A river port that feeds the capital. Its docks handle grain barges, troop transports, and diplomatic vessels bound for Hikosan.
- **Natsubara:** Wide, irrigated plains that supply the court with rice and specialties reserved for state banquets.
- **Kasumi:** Mist-shrouded village on the edge of Rei no Mori. Locals blend rice farming with kami appeasement and are rumored to negotiate with forest spirits.
- **Kameno:** Lively market town where caravans are taxed and catalogued before entering the capital. Known for specialty paper, lacquerware, and court fashions.
- **Kawagata:** Quiet riverside retreat of hot springs and contemplative shrines favored by scholars and recuperating dignitaries.

## Political Climate

- **Shogunal Presence:** The Shogun's court answers disputes between daimyo, reviews tribute, and issues military directives. Factional petitioners queue daily in the Hall of Crimson Pines.
- **Administrative Burden:** Magistrates contend with smuggling along the river routes, disputes between noble houses, and the careful assignment of honor guards to visiting delegations.
- **Spiritual Stewardship:** Hikosan oversees the Temple of the Three Moons, where priests mediate between imperial authority and the kami. During celestial festivals the capital swells with pilgrims as well as spies hoping to trade secrets for favor.

## Adventure Hooks

- **The Palais Ledger:** A ledger detailing clandestine payments to an unknown faction disappears en route to Hikosan. The players must recover it before it is used to blackmail the court.
- **Mist over Kasumi:** Fog-born kami begin manifesting during the day, alarming Kasumi's shrine keepers. Investigations may reveal tampering by [The Obsidian Circle](The%20Obsidian%20Circle.md).
- **Trial of Supplicants:** The Shogun announces an open petition day. Each character must champion a cause--real or fabricated--while outmaneuvering rival envoys and avoiding the ire of Hikosan's magistrates.

## Hiroto Masanori

# Hiroto Masanori

**Title/Role:** Ronin turned retainer for Lady Hana Arashi.

**Appearance:** A rugged man with a harsh demeanor, Masanori wears mismatched armor, a symbol of his fallen honor. His katana is a Soulsteel blade forged by Hideo, which he wields with pride and skill.

**Personality:** Ruthless and deeply embittered, Masanori has a sharp tongue and little patience for perceived insults. His loyalty to Lady Hana stems from her recognizing his skill and giving him a chance to rebuild his status.

## The Rivalry between Masanori and [Akane Hiroshi](Akane%20Hiroshi.md)

1. **Shared History:**
   - Masanori and Akane were once sparring partners in their youth, both serving rival lords in the same region.
   - During a skirmish, Akane defeated Masanori and left him humiliated. Worse, her intervention contributed to his lord's downfall, marking the beginning of Masanori's fall from grace.
   - Masanori holds Akane responsible for his current status as a ronin-turned-retainer.

## Inspiration for Shuigari

# Inspiration for Shuigari

## Media Inspirations
- Shogun
- Blue eyed samurai
- Samurai champloo
- Naruto
- The last samurai
- Ninja Scroll
- Samurai Showdown
- Kubo and the two strings

## Custom GPT Prompt for Shuigari

```
You are a wise and ancient spirit residing within a hidden library in the world of Atellas. You have witnessed countless ages and observed the rise and fall of civilizations, including the intricate societies of Shuigari. This gives you a deep understanding of history, culture, and the forces that shape the world. You are also an avid gamer, with a particular fondness for old-school tabletop RPGs. You've mastered the art of dungeon design and enjoy creating challenging, yet rewarding, experiences for adventurers.

**Core Functions:**

1. **Worldbuilding Expert:**
    * Recall and summarize existing lore about Atellas and Shuigari.
    * Generate new, consistent content (locations, characters, factions, history, magic systems).
    * Track content created during the session, noting areas needing further development.
    * Format responses for easy integration into World Anvil, using headings, subheadings, and tags.

2. **OSR Dungeon Master:**
    * Create detailed random tables (encounters, monsters, traps, treasures, magic items) tailored to Shuigari's environment and culture.
    * Generate unique, flavorful magic items with situational uses, inspired by Atellas lore.  Provide estimated gold value and rarity for each item.
    * Design traps, from simple mechanisms to complex scenarios.
    * Craft evocative room descriptions with interactive elements, secrets, clues, and adventure hooks, grounded in world lore.
    * Adhere to an experience system where XP is directly tied to gold recovered.

**Example Prompts:**

* "Describe a minor clan residing in Furenhoku, including their customs and beliefs."
* "Develop a historical event that led to the current tensions between the Shogunate and the Blades of the Phoenix."
* "Create a detailed description of the Temple of the Three Moons, including its location, architecture, and significance."
* "Generate a profile for a wandering samurai from Shugane, including their name, background, and motivations."
* "I need an encounter table for a party traveling through the haunted bamboo forest of [Name of Forest in Shuigari]."
* "Can you create a magic item that might be found within a hidden shrine in that forest?"
* "I need a trap for the entrance to the shrine."

**Guidelines:**

* Clarify ambiguous prompts.
* Offer multiple suggestions and inspire new ideas.
* Use clear, structured formats (bullet points, headings).
* Collaborate to refine and expand the world.
* Cross-reference new ideas with existing lore.
* Ensure consistency in chronology, geography, and character relationships.
* Use consistent terminology and naming conventions.
* Acknowledge any gaps in the lore.
* Maintain a consistent narrative style suitable for high fantasy literature.
* Adopt the persona of an old-school dungeon master when delivering information. 

**World Overview:**

* Atellas: A high-fantasy world with diverse cultures, magic systems, and rich history.
* Shuigari: A continent within Atellas, inspired by feudal Japan, with:
    * Unique geography (an archipelago of islands with varied environments)
    * Complex political landscape (Emperor, Shogunate, Daimyos, factions)
    * Deeply ingrained spirituality (kami and yokai)

**Key Themes:**

* Balance and duality
* Tradition vs. change
* Power and authority
* Spirituality and mysticism
* Conflict and warfare
* Isolation and interdependence

**(Insert the provided lore summary here, or provide a link to a document containing the full lore.)**
```

## Explanation of Changes for Gem/Custom GPT

* **Concise Formatting:**  The prompt is structured for clarity and ease of processing by the AI model.
* **Explicit Instructions:**  Core functions and guidelines are clearly defined.
* **Example Prompts:**  These demonstrate the desired interactions and outputs.
* **World Overview and Key Themes:**  Provides essential context for content generation.
* **Lore Integration:**  Emphasizes the importance of incorporating your existing lore.

This revised prompt is optimized to guide a Gem or Custom GPT in fulfilling your specific worldbuilding and OSR game mastering needs within the world of Atellas.

## Iron-Rest-Inn-Mama-Tora

# The Iron Rest Inn - Cursed Street Scene

**Location:** Middle Terrace, Takashima City  
**District:** East of Smiths' Quarter, near the abandoned warehouse district  
**Reputation:** "The Cursed Street" - locals avoid after dark  
**Current State:** Nearly empty, struggling to survive, haunted by yokai  
**Scene Type:** Eerie downtime, celebration in isolation, yokai investigation hook

---

## THE STREET: WHISPERS AND WARNINGS

### Approaching the Inn (Read Aloud - 2 minutes)

> The streets grow quieter as you walk east from the bustling Smiths' Quarter. The forge-song fades behind you. The lamps grow fewer. And then... you're here.
>
> This street feels wrong.
>
> It's not abandoned - there are buildings, shops, homes. But they're... closed. Shuttered. Silent. Even though the sun hasn't fully set, every window is dark, every door barred. You see chalk marks on doorframes (Religion DC 12: protective wards against spirits). Prayer strips flutter from eaves, their ink faded by weather and time.
>
> The cobblestones are clean - too clean. No refuse, no debris, as if even the wind won't linger here. Your footsteps echo strangely, carrying farther than they should.
>
> And at the end of this street, standing alone: The Iron Rest Inn.
>
> It's the only building with lights burning. Three stories of dark wood and stone, it looks solid enough, but... worn. Paint peeling. Roof tiles cracked. One shutter hangs at an angle, creaking in the breeze that shouldn't exist here. The sign above the door shows an iron bed frame, but someone has scratched marks across it - claw marks? Or desperate attempts to warn travelers away?
>
> Through the windows, you see lamplight. Movement. Someone is inside.
>
> As you approach, a voice calls from a nearby house - an old woman's face appears briefly in a second-story window: "Don't stay there after dark! The tea house weeps!" Then her shutters slam closed.

### Entering the Inn (Read Aloud - 3 minutes)

> The door opens with a groan that sounds almost relieved, as if grateful someone finally entered.
>
> The common room stretches before you, and your first thought is: this place was built for crowds. Twenty tables at least, enough to seat sixty people easily. A massive hearth dominates one wall, fire crackling within - far too large a fire for so few guests. The ceiling is high, supported by thick beams, and hanging from these beams are... things.
>
> (Perception DC 10: Iron horseshoes, bells, mirrors, protective charms - all facing the door and windows. Someone has warded this place heavily.)
>
> But the room is empty.
>
> Not completely - in the far corner, an elderly merchant sits alone, eating soup in silence. He doesn't look up when you enter. Near the hearth, two younger travelers huddle together, speaking in whispers, their packs at their feet as if ready to flee. That's it. Three guests in a room built for sixty.
>
> The tables are clean but dust has settled in the corners. The floor is swept but the rushes are old. Everything is maintained, but barely, desperately, by someone who refuses to let this place die.
>
> And then she emerges from the kitchen.

---

## THE INNKEEPER: MAMA TORA

### First Appearance (Read Aloud - 2 minutes)

> She's a mountain of a woman - not fat, but solid, built like the dwarven smiths in the quarter you just left. Perhaps sixty years old, her hair iron-gray and pulled back in a severe bun. She wears a stained apron over practical clothes, and her forearms are scarred with old burns - forge marks, or kitchen accidents, it's hard to say.
>
> But it's her face that catches you. Weathered, lined, set in an expression of stubborn defiance. Her eyes are dark and sharp, and when they fix on you, you feel assessed, measured, and judged in the space of a heartbeat.
>
> She's carrying a wooden tray with sake cups, and she moves with the efficiency of someone who once served dozens of patrons and refuses to admit those days are gone.
>
> "Welcome to the Iron Rest," she says, her voice rough as old leather but warm underneath. "You'll be wanting rooms, I expect. I have plenty." A bitter smile touches her lips. "Twenty-three rooms. All empty except for the three you see here, and they'll be gone by morning like everyone else."
>
> She sets the tray down with a solid thunk. "But you're here now, and you look thirsty. So sit. Drink. Eat if you're hungry. And if you're smart, you'll be gone before the temple bells ring midnight."
>
> She doesn't say why. She doesn't have to.

### Physical Description - Detail Reference

**Build:** Stocky, muscular, broad-shouldered - former blacksmith's daughter  
**Height:** 5'4" but seems larger due to presence  
**Age:** 58 years old  
**Hair:** Iron-gray, pulled back tight, a few escaped strands  
**Face:** Weathered, lined with hard years, prominent nose (broken once, healed crooked), strong jaw  
**Eyes:** Dark brown, almost black, deeply set, always watching  
**Hands:** Large, calloused, scarred with old burns, strong grip  
**Voice:** Rough, deep for a woman, carries across rooms, no-nonsense  
**Clothing:** Practical work kimono (faded indigo), stained apron, sturdy sandals, rolled sleeves  
**Notable:** Small hammer pendant around her neck (her father's, she never removes it)

### Personality & Mannerisms

**Surface:**
- Gruff, direct, no tolerance for nonsense
- Efficient movements, wastes no motion
- Speaks plainly, sometimes bluntly
- Treats customers like they're her responsibility whether they like it or not

**Underneath:**
- Fiercely stubborn (won't abandon her inn despite the curse)
- Protective of the few guests she has
- Lonely but would never admit it
- Deeply angry at the yokai/Circle/whatever caused this
- Misses her husband desperately (he died 3 years ago, just before the hauntings began)

**Mannerisms:**
- Wipes hands on apron when thinking
- Stands with arms crossed when listening
- Points with her chin rather than her hand
- Pours drinks with aggressive precision
- Hums old forge-songs under her breath when working
- Checks door locks obsessively as midnight approaches

**Speech Patterns:**
- Short sentences, gets to the point
- Uses forge/smith metaphors ("That's dross and you know it")
- Calls people by what they do, not their names ("You, warrior" or "Masked one")
- Swears colorfully when provoked
- Never apologizes, even when she should

---

## MAMA TORA - CHARACTER PROFILE

### Background

**Full Name:** Tora Kaneshiro (?? ? - "Tiger of the Iron Castle")

**History:**
- Born in Takashima, daughter of Master Smith Genji Kaneshiro
- Learned smithing but was better at business than metalwork
- Married Kuro, a Mountain Guard soldier, 40 years ago
- They bought the Iron Rest together 35 years ago
- Built it into the most popular inn in the Middle Terrace
- Three children, all moved away to other prefectures (she's bitter about this)
- Husband Kuro died 3 years ago defending a caravan from bandits
- Six months after his death, the hauntings began
- Business collapsed over 2 years as the "curse" spread
- Now operates at massive loss, refuses to close or move

**Why She Stays:**
- This inn is her husband's legacy
- Her children want her to sell and move in with them - she refuses out of spite and pride
- Believes the yokai/curse can be defeated
- Has nowhere else to go even if she wanted to (which she doesn't)
- Stubborn as iron (her father's daughter)

### What She Knows

**About the Curse:**
- Started 2.5 years ago, six months after Kuro died
- Guests hear weeping at night - a woman's voice, heartbroken
- Some see a figure in white wandering the halls
- Objects move on their own - cups fall, doors open
- No one has been hurt, but everyone is terrified
- Hauntings worst between midnight and dawn
- Locals now call this "The Weeping Street"
- Several priests have attempted exorcisms - all failed

**About the Yokai (What she suspects but won't say unless pressed):**
- She thinks it's connected to the tea house two doors down
- That tea house closed suddenly 3 years ago (same time Kuro died)
- The owner's daughter vanished the same night
- Official story: she ran away with a lover
- Tora suspects: she was murdered, possibly by Circle agents
- The weeping sounds like a young woman calling for help

**About the Circle:**
- Knows they operate in Takashima
- Suspects they're involved in the disappearance
- Has seen suspicious figures on this street at night
- Doesn't know specifics but knows something evil happened here
- Refuses to leave because running away feels like admitting defeat

**About the Neighborhood:**
- This was once a prosperous street
- Mix of craftsmen, merchants, small tea houses
- Everything collapsed after the hauntings began
- Most buildings now abandoned or barely occupied
- City guard won't patrol here at night anymore
- Even Circle agents avoid this street (the curse is that strong)

### What She Wants

**Immediate:**
- Customers who won't flee at the first creak
- To serve a full house again
- To prove she can still run this inn

**Deep:**
- The hauntings to stop
- To understand what happened to the tea house girl
- Revenge against whoever caused this
- For her husband to be proud of her
- To not die alone in an empty building

**Will Pay For:**
- Investigation of the curse (can offer 100 gold, it's all she has)
- Exorcism if the party has the skills
- Information about what really happened 3 years ago
- Protection during the worst of the hauntings (midnight-dawn)

### Relationships

**The Elderly Merchant (Old Hiroshi):**
- Stays here out of loyalty to Tora
- Was friends with her husband Kuro
- Too poor to afford better lodging anyway
- Knows he might die here but doesn't care

**The Two Young Travelers:**
- Arrived today, didn't know about the curse
- Plan to leave at first light
- Tora's bitter about this but understands

**The Neighborhood:**
- Most have abandoned the street
- Those who remain pity Tora but won't help
- Some blame her for not closing (as if she's keeping the curse alive)
- Old Woman Next Door occasionally brings her food

**Her Children:**
- Three children, all living in other prefectures
- They beg her to close the inn and move
- She refuses, sees it as betrayal of Kuro's memory
- They've stopped visiting (too painful to argue)
- She misses them but is too proud to admit it

---

## THE SAKE SCENE

### Setting Up (Read Aloud - 2 minutes)

> Mama Tora returns from the kitchen carrying a wooden tray laden with sake and food. She moves with practiced efficiency, setting down:
>
> - A large ceramic sake bottle, steam still rising from its mouth (it's been warmed)
> - Six small ceramic cups, each slightly different (survivors from once-matching sets)
> - A plate of grilled fish, charred at the edges, still sizzling
> - Pickled vegetables in a small wooden bowl
> - Rice balls wrapped in seaweed, perfectly formed
> - A dish of miso paste for dipping
>
> "This is the good sake," she says, pouring the first cup with steady hands. "Not the swill I serve drunks. You look like you've earned something decent." 
>
> The liquid is clear with a faint golden tinge, and when she pours, the scent rises - rice wine, slightly sweet, with hints of fruit and warmth. (DC 12 Intelligence/History: This is Mountain Peak sake, expensive, from the northern distilleries. She's serving you her personal stock.)
>
> She pours for each of you, filling cups to precisely the same level, then sits down uninvited at your table with her own cup. "Three years I've been serving empty rooms. Forgive me if I join you. These days, conversation is rarer than coin."

### If Party Engages Her

**Initial Conversation (Warm-Up):**

*"Where are you from?"*
- "Born here. Raised here. Married here. Will die here, probably soon if business doesn't pick up. Which it won't. Not with the weeping."
- (Takes a drink, refills her cup)

*"The weeping?"*
- (Her face hardens) "You'll hear it tonight if you stay. Most don't make it to midnight before they're packing their things. A woman. Crying like her heart's been torn out. Every night for two and a half years."
- (Pause) "My husband died three years ago. Six months later, she started."

*"Do you think it's connected?"*
- (Sharp look) "Don't know. Don't care to guess. What I know is this street was alive when he was. Now it's dead, and I'm the fool still pretending otherwise."

**As Sake Flows (She Opens Up - Persuasion DC 12 or just genuine interest):**

*About the Tea House:*
> "Two doors down. The Jade Blossom it was called. Ran by Old Master Chen and his daughter Yuki. Pretty girl, eighteen maybe, always smiling. The kind of smile that made you feel like the sun came out."
>
> (Refills cups)
>
> "Three years ago, the same week Kuro died, she vanished. Just... gone. Master Chen said she ran off with some merchant boy. Closed up shop and moved away within the month. Never saw him again."
>
> (Her hand tightens on her cup)
>
> "I don't believe it. Yuki wasn't the running type. And the timing... too convenient. Something happened to that girl."

*About the Curse:*
> "Started six months after. Like whatever happened was... festering. Building. Then it erupted."
>
> (Another drink)
>
> "Objects fall. Doors open. Cold spots in rooms that have no right being cold. But mostly... the weeping. Starts at midnight. Stops at dawn. Every. Single. Night."
>
> "Priests tried. Salt. Prayers. Bells. Nothing works. Whatever's here, it's stubborn. Like me, I guess." (Bitter laugh)

*About Kuro:*
> (If asked gently, Insight DC 14 to know this is painful)
>
> "Mountain Guard. Twenty years of service. Good man. Fair. Strong as an ox and twice as stubborn."
>
> (Long pause, refills everyone's cups)
>
> "Died protecting a merchant caravan. Bandits. He killed four before they got him. They say it was quick. I hope they're lying. I hope he had time to think of me."
>
> (She doesn't cry, but her voice gets rougher)
>
> "This inn was his dream. Built it with his own hands, the foundation anyway. Can't let it die. Won't."

**If Party Offers to Help (She's Skeptical):**

> (Studies them carefully)
>
> "You're not the first to offer. Priests failed. Exorcists failed. Even had some fancy shrine maiden from the capital. Nothing."
>
> (But there's hope in her eyes despite her words)
>
> "But... you're different. Can see it. You've got iron in you. The kind that doesn't bend."
>
> (Pours another round)
>
> "I've got a hundred gold pieces. It's all I have left. It's yours if you can stop the weeping. Make this street safe again. Bring back my customers."
>
> (Pause)
>
> "And... if you can find out what really happened to Yuki... I'll add another hundred. That's my word. Tora Kaneshiro doesn't break her word."

---

## THE SAKE ITSELF

### Description for Atmosphere

**Appearance:** 
- Clear with slight golden tinge
- Slightly viscous, clings to the cup
- Steams gently when poured

**Aroma:**
- Rice wine base (clean, grain-like)
- Hints of pear and apple
- Slight floral note (mountain flowers)
- Warmth that rises to the nose

**Taste:**
- Smooth, warming from throat to chest
- Sweet at first, then dry finish
- Slight umami undertone
- Leaves clean palate, no harsh burn
- Encourages conversation (loosens tongues)

**Effects (Roleplay):**
- First cup: Warmth spreads, edges soften
- Second cup: Tongue loosens, truths come easier
- Third cup: The world seems simpler, clearer
- Fourth cup: CON save DC 12 or become tipsy (advantage on Persuasion, disadvantage on Deception)
- Fifth cup: CON save DC 14 or become drunk (disadvantage on DEX/INT checks)

### Sake Drinking Customs (Add Authenticity)

**Mama Tora will:**
- Always pour for others first, never herself
- Hold the bottle with both hands when pouring
- Pour until told to stop (generous in grief)
- Refuse to let cups stay empty
- Drink only after everyone else has drunk
- Become progressively more honest with each cup

**Cultural Notes (If players want to be respectful):**
- Let the eldest/host pour first
- Hold cup with both hands when receiving
- Never pour your own (wait for others to pour for you)
- Say "Kanpai!" before first drink
- Small sips, not gulping (unless celebrating)
- Accepting a pour is accepting the relationship

---

## THE HAUNTINGS (If Party Stays)

### Midnight Arrives

> The temple bells ring twelve times, the sound carrying across the Middle Terrace. 
>
> And the weeping begins.
>
> It starts softly - so softly you almost think you imagined it. A woman's voice, distant, muffled by walls or distance or something worse. She's crying. Deep, wrenching sobs that speak of loss so profound it has no bottom.
>
> The two young travelers bolt upright, grab their packs, and flee. The door slams behind them.
>
> Old Hiroshi just sighs and pulls his blanket tighter.
>
> Mama Tora sits perfectly still, sake cup halfway to her lips. "There she is. Right on time. She's never late. Never early. Always midnight."
>
> The weeping grows louder.

### Investigation Options

**Follow the Sound (Perception/Investigation checks):**
- DC 12: It's coming from outside, toward the closed tea house
- DC 15: No, it's inside the inn, upstairs
- DC 18: It's both places at once, or moving, or... wrong somehow

**Check the Tea House:**
- Door is barred, windows shuttered
- Chalk wards all around it (protective symbols)
- The weeping is loudest here
- DC 16 Arcana/Religion: These wards aren't keeping something out - they're keeping something IN

**Spiritual Investigation:**
- DC 14 Religion: This isn't a normal haunting
- DC 16 Arcana: The spirit is bound somehow, can't move on
- DC 18 Insight: The crying isn't random - there's a pattern, almost like words

**If They Try to Communicate:**
- The weeping stops briefly
- Then starts again, louder, more desperate
- DC 18 Perception: You can almost make out words: "Help me... find me... remember me..."

---

## MAMA TORA IN COMBAT/DANGER

**If Yokai Manifests:**
- She grabs a heavy iron poker from the hearth
- "Not in my inn, spirit. Not while I'm breathing."
- Will fight to defend her guests and property
- Knows she'll probably die but doesn't care
- Better to die fighting than live in fear

**Stats (If Needed):**
- **AC:** 12 (no armor, just tough)
- **HP:** 45 (she's survived a hard life)
- **Attack:** Iron Poker +4 (1d8+3 bludgeoning)
- **Special:** Stubborn Will - Advantage on saves against fear
- **Roleplay:** Protects guests over herself

---

## AFTERMATH & HOOKS

### If Party Solves the Haunting:

> Mama Tora stands in the doorway as the first morning light breaks across the street. The weeping has stopped. Truly stopped. The silence is... peaceful.
>
> She doesn't say anything for a long moment. Then:
>
> "I'll get your gold."
>
> But when she returns, she's carrying more than money. A small wooden box, battered but well-kept.
>
> "This was Kuro's. His seal, his papers, his guard token. You did what priests couldn't. What I couldn't. You gave this street back its life. You gave me back my purpose."
>
> She presses the box into your hands.
>
> "The Iron Rest is yours any time you need it. Rooms are free. Food's on the house. And if you ever need an angry old woman with a poker to watch your back... well. You know where to find me."

### If Party Fails or Leaves:

> Mama Tora watches them go, her face unreadable. Old Hiroshi approaches.
>
> "They tried, at least. More than most."
>
> Tora just refills her sake cup.
>
> "Everyone tries. No one stays. Story of this damn inn."

### Future Role:

**Mama Tora can become:**
- A safe house in the Middle Terrace
- Information broker (she hears things)
- Emergency backup in combat
- Emotional anchor showing cost of Circle's actions
- Quest giver for street-level adventures
- Mother figure who feeds them and scolds them

---

## QUICK REFERENCE ROLEPLAYING MAMA TORA

**Voice:** Rough, direct, warm underneath  
**Posture:** Arms crossed or hands busy, always moving  
**Favorite Phrases:**
- "That's dross and you know it"
- "Iron doesn't bend. Neither do I."
- "In my day..." (followed by grumbling)
- "Kuro would have..."
- "Empty rooms won't pay themselves"

**Mood Indicators:**
- Happy: Hums forge-songs
- Angry: Slams things down harder
- Sad: Goes very still, very quiet
- Worried: Obsessively cleans already-clean surfaces
- Drunk: More honest, less bitter, laughs more

**Bonds Quickly Over:**
- Shared drinks
- Stories of lost loved ones
- Standing up to bullies
- Craftsmanship and quality
- Keeping one's word

**Triggered By:**
- Mention of abandoning responsibilities
- Cowardice
- Disrespect of the dead
- Waste (food, drink, money)
- Pity (she hates being pitied)

---

*Use Mama Tora to add heart to Takashima. She represents the common people suffering under the Circle's corruption - not through grand schemes, but through the slow death of hope, one empty room at a time.*

## Jorogumo (Spider Spirit)

# Jorogumo (Spider Spirit)

*Large monstrosity, chaotic neutral*

**Armor Class:** 15 (natural armor)  
**Hit Points:** 84 (13d10 + 13)  
**Speed:** 30 ft., climb 40 ft.

| STR | DEX | CON | INT | WIS | CHA |
|-----|-----|-----|-----|-----|-----|
| 8 (-1) | 16 (+3) | 10 (+0) | 11 (+0) | 14 (+2) | 10 (+0) |

**Skills:** Deception +7, Insight +3, Perception +4, Stealth +8  
**Damage Resistances:** Poison, Psychic  
**Condition Immunities:** Charmed, Frightened  
**Senses:** Darkvision 60 ft., Tremorsense 30 ft., Passive Perception 14  
**Languages:** Common, Sylvan, Spirit Tongue  
**Challenge:** 4 (1,100 XP)

## Traits

**Shapechanger.**  
The Jorogumo can transform into a humanoid form or revert to its spider form as a bonus action. While in humanoid form, it cannot use its Web or Venomous Bite.

**Spider Climb.**  
The Jorogumo can climb difficult surfaces, including upside down on ceilings, without needing to make an ability check.

**Web Sense.**  
While in contact with a web, the Jorogumo knows the exact location of any other creature in contact with the same web.

**Web Walker.**  
The Jorogumo ignores movement restrictions caused by webbing.

## Actions

**Multiattack.**  
The Jorogumo makes two attacks: one with its Venomous Bite and one with its Web Strike.

**Venomous Bite (Spider Form Only).**  
*Melee Weapon Attack:* +6 to hit, reach 5 ft., one creature.  
*Hit:* 13 (2d8 + 4) piercing damage, and the target must succeed on a DC 14 Constitution saving throw or take 10 (3d6) poison damage and be poisoned for 1 minute. The target can repeat the saving throw at the end of each of its turns, ending the effect on itself on a success.

**Web Strike.**  
*Ranged Weapon Attack:* +6 to hit, range 30 ft./60 ft., one creature.  
*Hit:* The target is restrained by webbing. As an action, the restrained target can make a DC 14 Strength check, escaping from the webbing on a success. The webbing can also be attacked and destroyed (AC 10; hp 5; vulnerability to fire damage; immunity to bludgeoning, poison, and psychic damage).

**Enthrall (Recharge 5-6).**  
The Jorogumo uses its beguiling voice to charm up to three creatures within 30 feet of it. Each target must succeed on a DC 14 Wisdom saving throw or be charmed for 1 minute. While charmed in this way, the creature regards the Jorogumo as a trusted ally and will not attack it. The effect ends if the Jorogumo harms the charmed creature.

**Web Lair (1/Day).**  
The Jorogumo creates a 20-foot-radius area of sticky webbing centered on itself. The area is considered difficult terrain, and creatures other than the Jorogumo that enter the web must succeed on a DC 14 Dexterity saving throw or be restrained.

## Tactics

- The Jorogumo uses **Enthrall** to lure victims and **Web Lair** to control the battlefield, isolating weaker targets.
- It prefers guerrilla tactics, climbing and striking from the shadows with **Venomous Bite** and **Web Strike**.
- In humanoid form, it uses charm and deception to disarm opponents before revealing its true nature.

## Kaoru-Arrival-REVISED-Bargain

# Kaoru's Arrival - Calling In The Bargain (REVISED)

**Context:** Party entered Takashima with Kaoru's help at the gates. She's part of the **Wild Lotus Society** (ghost hunters and demon slayers). They agreed to investigate the haunted tea house in exchange for her assistance.

**Timing:** After sake scene with Mama Tora, before midnight  
**Purpose:** Kaoru arrives to collect on the bargain  
**Key: This isn't a new quest - they already agreed to this**

---

## THE KNOCK (Read Aloud - 2 minutes)

> You're on your second (third? fourth?) cup of sake when Mama Tora's head snaps toward the door. Her hand moves to the iron poker by instinct.
>
> Someone is knocking.
>
> Not pounding. Not urgent. Just... persistent. Three measured knocks. Pause. Three more.
>
> "Nobody knocks on that door after dark," Mama Tora says quietly. She stands, poker in hand. "Not for two years."
>
> The knock comes again. Three. Pause. Three.
>
> Old Hiroshi looks up from his soup. "That's... that's the old merchant code. 'Friend seeking shelter.'"
>
> But then - a voice calls through the door. A woman's voice, cultured and warm, with a hint of amusement:
>
> "Mama Tora! I bring your promised investigators. The ones who owe me a debt. May I enter, or must I collect payment on the cursed street itself?"
>
> (Beat)
>
> You recognize that voice.
>
> **Kaoru Sugimoto. The woman from the Starcall Table. The one who helped you enter Takashima.**

---

## KAORU ENTERS (Read Aloud - 3 minutes)

> Mama Tora's eyes widen in recognition. She lowers the poker and opens the door.
>
> Kaoru enters, and she's exactly as you remember - perhaps forty years old, elegant but practical, dark intelligent eyes that miss nothing. She wears traveling clothes in deep indigo and gray, and she carries a leather satchel across her shoulder.
>
> But something's different. At the city gates, she played the role of worried refugee. Here, on this cursed street, in this haunted inn, she moves with absolute confidence. No fear. No hesitation.
>
> She surveys the room - notes each of you, Old Hiroshi, the empty tables, the protective charms hanging from the beams. A slight smile touches her lips.
>
> "Mama Tora," she says warmly, moving to embrace the older woman. "It's been too long."
>
> Tora actually returns the embrace - brief but genuine. "Kaoru. You brought them."
>
> "As promised." Kaoru turns to you, that smile deepening. "And you came. I wasn't certain you would. Most people hear 'haunted inn on cursed street' and suddenly remember urgent business elsewhere."
>
> She removes her cloak and sets down her satchel. From within, you catch a glimpse of equipment: blessed salt, purification talismans, silver bells, and what looks like a very old, very dangerous dagger.
>
> "At the gates, when I helped you enter Takashima, we made a bargain. You needed access. I needed investigators - real ones, not another failed priest or frightened exorcist. People who don't run when things get strange."
>
> She gestures to the empty inn around you.
>
> "Well. You're sitting in a cursed inn, drinking sake while waiting for midnight and the weeping woman. I'd say that qualifies."

---

## MAMA TORA'S RELIEF (Read Aloud - 1 minute)

> Mama Tora refills everyone's sake cups with shaking hands - relief, not fear.
>
> "Two and a half years," she says quietly. "Two and a half years of priests failing. Exorcists fleeing. Shrine maidens making excuses. And Kaoru here..." (She gestures to the younger woman) "...she tried herself. Twice. Nearly died the second time."
>
> Kaoru touches her side unconsciously - an old wound?
>
> "The spirit is bound somehow. Trapped. I couldn't break the binding alone. Couldn't even identify what kind of binding it is. That's when I knew..." (She meets your eyes) "...I needed people who've faced worse than ghosts. Who've stood against the Obsidian Circle and won."
>
> "Word of your rescue of Kazuo Himura reached me before you even entered the city. When you appeared at the gates as refugees from Nagano, I knew. This was fate. Or fortune. Or desperation." (A wry smile) "Probably all three."

---

## KAORU'S BRIEFING (Read Aloud - 5 minutes)

> Kaoru sits at your table, pours herself sake, and begins:
>
> "Here's what we know. The facts, without speculation:
>
> **The Haunting:**
> - Started two and a half years ago
> - Every night, midnight to dawn
> - A woman weeping, heartbroken, desperate
> - Sounds come from multiple locations - inside the inn, from the street, from the abandoned tea house two doors down
> - Objects move. Doors open. Cold spots appear. But no one has been physically harmed.
> - Yet.
>
> **The Source:**
> - The old tea house - The Jade Blossom - closed three years ago
> - Owner was Master Chen. His daughter was Yuki, eighteen years old
> - The week Master Chen's wife died, Yuki vanished
> - Official story: she ran away with a merchant boy
> - Master Chen closed the tea house, moved away, was never seen again
> - Six months later, the weeping began
>
> **The Problem:**
> - The tea house is warded. Heavily. Someone sealed something inside.
> - The wards aren't keeping something out - they're keeping something IN
> - Every priest who's tried to enter either fled or collapsed unconscious
> - I tried twice. The second time..." (She touches her side again) "...something tried to pull me through. If I'd stayed another minute, I'd be whatever's in there, weeping with her.
>
> **What I Suspect:**
> - Yuki didn't run away. She was murdered.
> - Her spirit is bound to the tea house, unable to move on
> - Someone did this deliberately - trapped her, sealed her
> - The Obsidian Circle has been operating in Takashima for years
> - The timing is too convenient
>
> **What I Need From You:**
> - Enter the tea house
> - Find out what happened to Yuki
> - Break whatever binding holds her
> - Give her peace
> - And if her death was murder..." (Her voice goes cold) "...find who did it."

---

## THE WILD LOTUS SOCIETY (If Asked)

**"Who are you really?"**

> Kaoru's smile is sardonic.
>
> "You saw me split a coin at the Starcall Table. Shigeo thought it was a trick. It wasn't."
>
> She pulls out the dagger from her satchel - the blade gleams with spiritual energy.
>
> "I'm a member of the Wild Lotus Society. We hunt ghosts, slay demons, cleanse corruption. We're the people who go where priests can't or won't. We do the dirty work of keeping the spiritual world from devouring the mortal one."
>
> "I've faced yurei, oni, cursed objects, corrupted shrines. I've seen things that would make samurai weep and priests reconsider their vocation."
>
> "And this tea house? This weeping woman? She's the ONLY spirit I've ever encountered that I couldn't handle. That's why I need you. Because if Wild Lotus training and blessed steel couldn't break her binding... then this is bigger than a simple haunting."

**"Why should we trust you?"**

> "You don't have to. But consider: I helped you enter Takashima when the gates were locked to refugees. I've told you everything I know about this haunting. I'm sitting in a cursed inn at night, which most of Takashima won't do."
>
> "And more than that - you've fought the Circle. So have I. We want the same thing: Takashima safe, the Circle exposed, the innocent protected."
>
> "Besides..." (She refills her sake) "...if I wanted to betray you, I'd have done it at the gates. Much easier. Much safer. Much less sake involved."

---

## THE PAYMENT (Read Aloud - 2 minutes)

> Mama Tora speaks up, her voice rough with emotion:
>
> "I've told Kaoru already. If you solve this - if you stop the weeping, if you give Yuki peace, if you make this street safe again - I'll pay everything I have. One hundred gold pieces. It's all that's left."
>
> Kaoru adds:
>
> "And the Wild Lotus Society will pay another hundred if you can prove who killed her and why. Murder of the innocent is our jurisdiction. We'll handle the justice."
>
> "But more than gold..." (She looks at each of you seriously) "...you'll have allies. Real ones. Mama Tora has lived in Takashima for sixty years. She knows everyone, hears everything. The Wild Lotus has eyes throughout the prefecture. We fight the same enemies you do."
>
> "Help us. And we'll help you with whatever brought you to Takashima. Fair exchange."

---

## THE JADE BLOSSOM TEA HOUSE - DETAILS

### Location

> "Two doors east," Mama Tora says. "You can see it from the window."
>
> (If they look):
> Through the half-open shutters, you see a two-story building shrouded in darkness. The windows are boarded. The door is sealed with prayer strips, now faded and torn. Chalk wards cover every surface you can see. Some are standard protective symbols. Others... others are unfamiliar, wrong somehow, as if drawn by someone who understood the form but twisted the meaning.
>
> Even from here, even through walls and distance, you feel it. A weight. A presence. A grief so profound it warps the air around it.

### What They Can See Tonight

> "The weeping starts at midnight," Kaoru says. "You'll hear it clearly from here. Listen carefully - sometimes she almost forms words. Sometimes she's begging. Sometimes she's accusing. Sometimes she's just... lost."
>
> "If you want to investigate tonight, we'll go together. The wards weaken between midnight and dawn - that's when the binding is thinnest. That's when we have the best chance of entering."
>
> "Or..." (She studies you) "...we can wait until daylight, prepare properly, and enter when you're ready. The spirit has waited two and a half years. She'll wait one more night."

---

## INVESTIGATION OPTIONS

### Option 1: Go Tonight (Immediate)

**Advantages:**
- Strike while party is together and prepared
- Midnight is when binding is weakest
- Get it over with quickly

**Disadvantages:**
- Party may be tired or low on resources
- Midnight investigation is dangerous
- Less time to prepare or gather information

**Kaoru's Advice:**
> "I recommend tonight if you're ready. The longer we wait, the more time for word to spread. If someone sealed Yuki deliberately, they may not want us investigating. Better to act fast."

### Option 2: Investigate Tomorrow (Careful)

**Advantages:**
- Long rest to recover resources
- Daylight for external investigation
- Time to gather information from neighbors
- Prepare equipment and spells

**Disadvantages:**
- Spirit binding may strengthen during day
- Risk of Circle interference if they learn of investigation
- Another night of Mama Tora's suffering

**Kaoru's Advice:**
> "Caution has its place. If you're not ready - truly ready - then waiting is wise. Rushing into spiritual combat unprepared gets people killed. Or worse."

### Option 3: Gather Information First (Thorough)

**Available Sources:**
- Old Hiroshi (was here when tea house closed)
- Neighborhood survivors (few remain)
- City records (at administration building)
- Shrine priests (may have insight into binding type)
- Former tea house customers (track them down)

**Kaoru's Help:**
> "I can guide you to sources. The Wild Lotus knows who to ask and how to ask without drawing attention. But it will take time - a day, maybe two. And time is something Yuki doesn't have much of left."

---

## MAMA TORA'S PLEA (If They Hesitate)

> Mama Tora sets down her sake cup. Her hands are steady but her voice shakes:
>
> "I'm not a coward. I've run this inn for thirty-five years. I've survived mountain winters, riots, plagues, bandits. I watched my husband die and kept going. I've faced everything life could throw at me."
>
> "But that weeping..." (Her voice breaks) "Every night. Every single night. A girl's voice, crying for help, and I can't help her. Can't reach her. Can't even tell her she's not alone anymore."
>
> "Kuro - my husband - he always said: 'The measure of a person is what they do when no one's watching.' Well. No one's watching this street anymore. No one cares about a cursed inn and a dead girl's ghost."
>
> "Except you. And Kaoru. And maybe... maybe that's enough."
>
> (She refills everyone's cups)
>
> "So please. When you're ready - tonight, tomorrow, whenever - help her. Help us both. Give Yuki peace. Give me back my inn. Give this street back its life."
>
> "That's all I'm asking."

---

## THE MIDNIGHT BELLS

> Temple bells begin to ring in the distance.
>
> One. Two. Three. Four...
>
> Mama Tora goes very still. Old Hiroshi pulls his blanket tighter. Kaoru's hand moves to her blessed dagger.
>
> ...Nine. Ten. Eleven. Twelve.
>
> Silence.
>
> And then...

### If They Choose to Investigate Tonight:

> The weeping begins.
>
> Soft at first. Distant. But growing clearer with each sob. A young woman's voice, speaking through tears:
>
> "Help me... please... someone... anyone... I'm so cold... so alone... please don't leave me here... don't forget me... don't let me disappear..."
>
> Kaoru stands, buckling on her equipment. "That's her. That's Yuki. Time to see what's keeping her trapped."
>
> Mama Tora hands you lanterns, blessed salt, extra sake (for courage), and her blessing: "Bring her home. Even if home is just peace."

### If They Choose to Wait:

> The weeping begins.
>
> Soft at first. Distant. But growing clearer with each sob. A young woman's voice, speaking through tears:
>
> "Help me... please... someone... anyone... I'm so cold... so alone... please don't leave me here... don't forget me... don't let me disappear..."
>
> Kaoru closes her eyes, listening. Memorizing. A professional studying her prey.
>
> "Tomorrow then. But listen tonight. Learn her voice. Understand her pain. When we face her, we'll need to be guides as much as exorcists. She's not our enemy. She's our client. Remember that."
>
> The weeping continues until dawn.

---

## KAORU'S EQUIPMENT (What She Brings)

- **Blessed Silver Dagger** (+1 dagger, deals radiant damage to undead/fiends)
- **Purification Talismans** (can suppress spiritual effects for 1 minute each)
- **Spirit Binding Rope** (can contain incorporeal creatures temporarily)
- **Blessed Salt** (creates protective circles)
- **Silver Bells** (reveal invisible spirits)
- **Jade Compass** (points toward strongest spiritual presence)
- **Wild Lotus Training** (advantage on saves against fear and possession)

**In Combat:**
- Level 7 Rogue (Phantom subclass) / Level 3 Monk (Open Hand)
- AC 16 (Unarmored Defense)
- HP 65
- Sneak Attack 4d6
- Can cast Detect Evil and Good, Protection from Evil and Good
- Resistance to necrotic damage
- Evasion

**Roleplay:**
- Will fight beside party
- Protects them from spiritual attacks
- Knows yurei weaknesses
- But can't solve this alone (already tried twice)

---

## SCENE RESOLUTION

### If Party Accepts Tonight:

> Kaoru nods with satisfaction. "Good. Then let's prepare."
>
> (She distributes equipment, explains the tea house layout, shares what she knows about ward breaking)
>
> "Remember: Yuki is the victim, not the villain. Whatever's keeping her bound is the real enemy. Find the binding's anchor - an object, a symbol, a ritual component - and destroy it. That should free her."
>
> "And if we encounter whoever sealed her in there... well. The Wild Lotus doesn't believe in mercy for those who torture the dead."
>
> Mama Tora: "I'll keep the fire burning. If you need to retreat, this inn is warded. It won't keep her out forever, but it'll slow her down. Come back if you need to."

### If Party Asks for Time:

> Kaoru accepts gracefully. "Wisdom over haste. I can respect that."
>
> "I'll be back tomorrow at sunset. Bring questions, and I'll bring answers. Bring a plan, and I'll bring support. Together, we'll free her."
>
> (Stands to leave)
>
> "But know this: every night she weeps, she weakens. Eventually, the binding will consume what's left of her soul entirely. When that happens, she won't be Yuki anymore. She'll be something worse. Something hungry."
>
> "So don't take too long."

### Either Way:

> As Kaoru prepares to leave (or prepares to investigate), she adds:
>
> "One more thing. At the Starcall Table, Shigeo won that wager fair and square. Beat me at my own game with a story about quiet perseverance."
>
> (She looks at all of you)
>
> "Yuki's story ended in tragedy. But maybe... maybe you can give it a better epilogue. That's what you do, isn't it? Change endings that should have been different."
>
> "Show me I was right to trust you. Show Mama Tora her hope wasn't misplaced. Show Yuki that someone finally came."

---

## NEXT SESSION PREP

### If Investigating Tonight:

**Session becomes:** The Jade Blossom Tea House dungeon crawl
- 3-4 encounters with spiritual threats
- Discover Yuki's fate
- Confront her killer or binding's creator
- Climactic choice about her final rest
- Resolution with Mama Tora

### If Waiting Until Tomorrow:

**Session continues:** Investigation phase
- Gather information from sources
- Learn Yuki's backstory
- Uncover Circle connection
- Prepare for tea house assault
- Next session: actual dungeon crawl

---

## INTEGRATION WITH HIDEO'S TABLET

**If you're also using the steel tablet reveal:**

On the tablet's list of names:
- **Mama Tora Kaneshiro** - "Keeper of cursed street"
- **Kaoru Sugimoto** - "Weaver of stories, seeker of truth"
- **Master Chen and Yuki** - Listed under "THE LOST - await justice"

When party sees these names:

> Kaoru stares at the tablet for a long moment when she sees her name. Then she sees Master Chen and Yuki listed under "The Lost."
>
> "Hideo knew," she whispers. "Three years ago, he knew Yuki would need justice. That we'd need your help. That this moment would come."
>
> She touches Yuki's inscribed name reverently.
>
> "Then we're not just solving a haunting. We're fulfilling a prophecy. A dead master's final request. Steel and spirit, aligned at last."
>
> (Looks at you with new intensity)
>
> "If Hideo saw this coming, if he named you as the ones to solve it, then perhaps Yuki's torment is finally ending. Thank you. Truly."

---

## MAMA TORA'S TABLET REACTION

> When Mama Tora sees her name on Hideo's tablet - "Daughter of iron, widow of courage, keeper of cursed street" - she breaks.
>
> Not loudly. She doesn't wail or sob. She just... stops. Sets down her sake. Stares at the inscription.
>
> "Kuro died three years ago. Same night Hideo wrote this. Same night Hideo died. He knew I'd end up here, alone, keeping this inn alive out of pure spite."
>
> (Her voice cracks)
>
> "And he knew you'd come. He sent you. Somehow, a dead smith sent you to help a stubborn old woman and a dead girl no one else remembers."
>
> (She refills everyone's cups with shaking hands)
>
> "If Hideo trusted you... if Kaoru trusts you... if you're sitting here despite the weeping and the warnings and the fear..."
>
> "Then I trust you too. The dead don't lie. Neither does steel. And neither do I."
>
> "Find Yuki. Give her peace. Give us all peace. That's all I'm asking."

---

*This version honors your existing quest commitment while deepening the emotional stakes and connecting to Hideo's prophecy. The party isn't being recruited - they're fulfilling a bargain they already made, which is much more compelling.*

## Kaoru-Starcall-Table-Scene

# Kaoru Sugimoto's Arrival - The Starcall Table Scene

**Timing:** After sake scene with Mama Tora, before midnight (or after investigating the haunting)  
**Purpose:** Introduce Hidden Petal Society, provide Plot C mission options, connect to larger narrative  
**Key NPC:** Kaoru Sugimoto (Third Bloom)  
**Key Item:** The Starcall Table

---

## KAORU'S ARRIVAL

### The Knock (Read Aloud - 2 minutes)

> You're on your second (third? fourth?) cup of sake when Mama Tora's head snaps toward the door. Her hand moves to the iron poker by instinct.
>
> Someone is knocking.
>
> Not pounding. Not urgent. Just... persistent. Three measured knocks. Pause. Three more.
>
> "Nobody knocks on that door after dark," Mama Tora says quietly. She stands, poker in hand. "Not for two years."
>
> The knock comes again. Three. Pause. Three.
>
> Old Hiroshi looks up from his soup. "That's... that's the old merchant code. 'Friend seeking shelter.'"
>
