## Named NPCs to Date
| Category | Notables |
| --- | --- |
| **Major Figures** | Master Hideo Nakata (late forgemaster), the boy heir (grandson), Kaien "Smiling Storm" (Circle general), Renji Saito (fisherman with Circle ties), Haruto (smith apprentice), Kadei (monk guardian), Kazuo Himura (retainer), Korha (Circle druid). |
| **Noble Houses** | Lady Ryoko Arashi (Daimyo of Takashima), Lady Hana Arashi (concubine), Lord Ichiro Kagamura (political rival). |
| **Circle Members** | Koruzu Tran (hexbinder), Vel Tain (scribe), Rakuya Saan (shadeblade captain), Mioshi (young recruit). |
| **Spirits & Entities** | Minari (Ashen Widow), Masaru (Ash-Tongue), Murakage (murmured presence). |

## Quick Generation Tips
- Combine a first name with a descriptive surname to hint at origin or role: *Takumi Shadowbrook* (scout), *Yuki Raincaller* (shrine maiden), *Shin Ironshell* (guard captain), *Nozomi Moonwatch* (oracle), *Riku Thornwatch* (ranger).
- Nobility often favour traditional surnames (Arashi, Kagamura). Commoners or guild members may adopt nature- or profession-linked surnames.
- Kami-touched or spiritual NPCs can carry evocative surnames (Sunwhisper, Ghostpine) to suggest connections beyond the mortal realm.

Keep this list handy during prep or at the table to maintain naming consistency across Shuigari.

## Aiko Hayashi

# Aiko Hayashi

## Overview
A quiet, intense member of [[The Starcall Table]] gambling group, notable for being the only member who appears to be an active warrior. Her travel-worn armor suggests she's seen real combat, making her an unusual addition to a group of gamblers and merchants.

## Appearance
- Wears travel-worn armor that shows signs of use
- Carries herself with the bearing of someone trained in combat
- Quiet and observant demeanor
- Intense presence despite her silence

## Personality
- Quiet and reserved, especially compared to her companions
- Intense and focused
- Observant - watches more than she speaks
- Professional and composed
- Knows her limits and when to fold

## Notable Moments
During the storytelling wager between [[Shigeo]] and [[Kaoru Sugimoto]], Aiko simply shook her head and folded without fanfare. Her quiet assessment of the situation showed the same tactical awareness she likely brings to combat - know when you're outmatched and conserve your resources.

## Mysteries
- What brings a warrior to travel with a gambling circle?
- Is she providing security for the group, or is gambling simply another challenge she enjoys?
- Her combat experience seems significant - what battles has she fought?
- Why does someone who appears to be a capable fighter spend her time at card tables on the Silver Road?

## Connections
**The Starcall Table:**
- [[Kaoru Sugimoto]] - The elegant merchant and skilled storyteller
- [[Daisuke "Silver Pockets" Inoue]] - The charming younger gambler
- [[Haruki "The Boar" Takeda]] - The boisterous drinker

## Campaign Notes
- First encountered: Along the Silver Road during the caravan journey to Toriyama
- Witnessed the famous storytelling wager
- Continues to travel with the Starcall Table
- Her presence suggests the group might face dangers that require armed protection

## Kaoru Sugimoto

## Overview
## Overview
A wealthy merchant who travels alone, part of the Starcall Table gambling group encountered during the caravan journey along the Silver Road to Toriyama. Despite her understated appearance, she possesses more wealth than her companions and carries herself with effortless composure and quiet confidence.

**Hidden Identity:** Member of the [[Wild Lotus Society]], a group of ghost hunters and demon slayers. Her merchant persona and gambling activities serve as cover for her supernatural investigations.## Appearance & Demeanor
- Simple but elegant attire suited to someone used to refinement
- Hands move gracefully, particularly when handling cards
- Calm, measured speech with understated confidence that draws attention without demanding it
- More refined and wealthy than she initially appears

## Personality Traits
- Appreciates stories and acts that challenge convention
- Bold wagerer, willing to take risks
- Competitive but gracious in defeat
- Theatrical and dramatic in her presentations
- Values precision and skill
- Carries herself with quiet authority

## Notable Encounters
## Notable Encounters

*For the full story of the Starcall Table encounter, see [[The Starcall Table]]*

### The Storytelling Wager (Night before the Great Stone Gate)
During a night at the Starcall Table, Shigeo wagered the Great Tortoise charm in a storytelling contest. Kaoru immediately wagered 100 gold kobans, taking the bait while others at the table folded.

**The Contest:**
- Shigeo told the tale of Atsuko, a woman from Kagemura who defied tradition to open trade routes - a grounded, believable story of quiet perseverance
- Kaoru countered with the story of Chiyo, the "Weaver of Blades" - a weaver's daughter who became a legendary warrior capable of commanding the wind itself
- Shigeo won by emphasizing believability and humility over Kaoru's more fantastical tale

**The Coin Trick:**
After conceding defeat graciously, Kaoru performed a dramatic flourish - flipping a gold koban into the air and miming a slicing motion with her hand. The coin landed on the table in two perfectly clean halves, leaving the crowd questioning whether it was sleight of hand or something more.

Her parting words: "I promise that next time, I'll not be so easily swayed. I've always liked stories with dramatic endings, haven't you?"
## Abilities & Skills
- Expert card player with remarkable dexterity
- Possibly possesses supernatural martial abilities (the coin-splitting display)
- Keen judge of character and situations
- Skilled storyteller

## Connections
## Connections
**The Starcall Table members:**
- [[Daisuke "Silver Pockets" Inoue]] - younger, charming gambler with silver bangles
- [[Haruki "The Boar" Takeda]] - broad-shouldered, boisterous drinker
- [[Aiko Hayashi]] - quiet, intense woman with travel-worn armor

*For the full story of their encounter with Shigeo, see [[The Starcall Table]]*
## Speculation & Mysteries
- The precision of her coin-splitting trick suggests either incredible sleight of hand or actual supernatural ability
- Her story of Chiyo the "Weaver of Blades" may have been more autobiographical than it appeared
- Her true wealth and purpose for traveling alone on the Silver Road remain unclear
- Is she simply a skilled merchant, or is there more to her identity?

## Campaign Notes
## Campaign Notes
- First encountered: Caravan journey to Toriyama, along the Silver Road
- Lost 100 gold kobans to Shigeo in the storytelling wager
- Left a strong impression on Shigeo with her theatrical display
- Shigeo feels curiosity about whether her abilities are real or illusion
- **Arc 2:** Reunited with party at the gates of Takashima, appearing as a refugee with the Starcall Table survivors from Nagano
- Revealed membership in the [[Wild Lotus Society]]
- Bargained party's entry into Takashima in exchange for investigating the haunted tea house
- Her supernatural abilities (coin trick) now explained by Wild Lotus training

## Kazuya

# Kazuya - The Brother

## Identity & Roles

**Full Name:** Kazuya [Family name - brother of the collections officer Iseri killed]

**Multiple Identities:**
1. **The Vengeful Brother** - Seeking revenge for Iseri's first kill
2. **Akemi's Killer** - Murdered Akemi Takahashi to protect secrets
3. **Fox's Former Comrade** - Member of Fox's assassin group/organization

## The Triple Threat

Kazuya represents the convergence of three PC storylines:
- **Iseri's nemesis** - The brother who wants revenge
- **Kanji's target** - The one who killed Akemi
- **Fox's betrayer** - Someone who knows Fox's true identity

## Background

### The Collections Officer Brother
- Kazuya's brother was a collections officer killed by Iseri at age 19
- The brother may have been corrupt, or simply doing his job
- Kazuya believes his brother was murdered by a vigilante criminal
- His quest for revenge has consumed him

### The Assassin
Kazuya is (or was) part of Fox's group, which means:
- He knows Fox's fighting style, weaknesses, methods
- He may know Fox's real face and name
- He has assassin skills comparable to Fox
- He understands how Fox thinks and operates

**Questions:**
- Is Kazuya still officially part of Fox's group?
- Did he leave? Go rogue? Is he deep cover?
- Does the group know what he's doing in Takashima?
- Is Fox's group hunting him, or do they support his mission?

### Akemi's Murder

**Why Kazuya Killed Akemi:**
Akemi Takahashi was investigating something in Takashima - likely:
1. The vault beneath the city
2. The Oniwaban massacre
3. Circle infiltration
4. Connection between all of the above

Akemi got too close to the truth. Kazuya killed them to:
- Protect the vault's location (for his own purposes)
- Prevent exposure of Circle plans (if he's working with them)
- Eliminate a witness to something specific
- Create a "accident" that wouldn't draw attention

**The Method:**
- Made it look like an accident (convincingly enough to fool most)
- But left clues that a skilled investigator (Kanji) could find
- Professional work - assassin's kill, not a crime of passion
- Clean enough that authorities closed the case

## Current Situation in Takashima

### Position & Resources
Kazuya has established himself in Takashima with:
- Wealth or position (how did he gain this?)
- Intelligence network
- Connections to key figures
- Ability to manipulate events

**Possible Covers:**
- Merchant guild member
- Minor noble or official
- Information broker
- Underground network leader
- Legitimate businessman with secret activities

### The Orchestrator

Kazuya is setting multiple events in motion:

**Against Iseri:**
- Arranged the troll attack on the road
- Knows Iseri is traveling with a party
- Plans to use the party to access the vault
- Wants Iseri to suffer before dying

**Using the Party:**
- Manipulating circumstances to drive them toward the vault
- May have influenced Kaoru's "coincidental" appearance
- Creating situations that force party to investigate
- Intends to use them to bypass vault defenses

**The Vault Plan:**
- Needs something from the vault (power? Artifact? Knowledge?)
- Can't access it alone (needs party? Needs specific people?)
- Willing to work with/manipulate Circle if necessary
- Has his own agenda separate from Circle's goals

## Relationship to Tenka (The Traitor Oniwaban)

Kazuya and Tenka are **counterparts** - parallel but separate villains:

**Similarities:**
- Both betrayed their "families" (Fox's group / Oniwaban clan)
- Both serve their own agendas while appearing to serve others
- Both have specialized combat skills
- Both want access to the vault
- Both are manipulating the party

**Differences:**
- Kazuya is driven by personal revenge (brother's death)
- Tenka was driven by [to be determined - survival? Ambition? Corruption?]
- Kazuya kills to protect secrets
- Tenka killed to gain secrets

**Potential Alliance:**
Do Kazuya and Tenka work together? Options:
1. **Yes** - They're allied, making them more dangerous
2. **No** - They're rivals, which party could exploit
3. **Complicated** - They use each other but don't trust each other
4. **Unknown** - One doesn't know the other exists yet

## Connection to Fox's Group

### The Revelation
When Fox discovers Kazuya is in Takashima, multiple revelations hit:

1. **Someone from home** - First person from Fox's past to appear
2. **He knows who Fox really is** - Under the mask
3. **He's Akemi's killer** - This means Fox's group is involved somehow
4. **He might expose Fox** - To the party, to authorities, to enemies

### The Questions

**About Fox's Group:**
- What is this organization? (Name, purpose, structure)
- Are they good, evil, or morally gray?
- Why was Fox part of them?
- Does Fox want to return, or are they running away?

**About Kazuya's Standing:**
- Did the group send Kazuya to Takashima?
- Is he rogue/expelled for killing Akemi?
- Does the group know about his revenge quest?
- Will other members arrive looking for Fox or Kazuya?

**About Fox's Position:**
- Does Fox still consider themselves part of the group?
- Will Fox defend Kazuya to the party despite everything?
- Must Fox choose between old loyalty and new friends?
- Can Fox kill a former brother-in-arms?

## Combat Capabilities

As an assassin from Fox's group:

**Skills:**
- Stealth and infiltration
- Poison expertise
- Intelligence gathering
- Social manipulation
- Close-quarters combat
- Tactical planning

**Advantages Over Party:**
- Knows Fox's abilities and counters them
- Has observed Iseri's vigilante patterns
- Professional killer vs. brawlers and adventurers
- Prepared battlefield, chooses when/where to fight
- Resources and backup in Takashima

**Weaknesses:**
- Emotional (revenge-driven, not purely rational)
- Arrogant (believes he's smarter than the party)
- Divided attention (multiple plots/enemies)
- Potential exposure (the more he acts, the more clues he leaves)

## Personality & Motivation

### The Mask of Civility
In public, Kazuya is:
- Charming and sophisticated
- Appears reasonable and cultured
- Hides his rage behind etiquette
- Uses politics and manipulation over obvious force

### The Core of Rage
Underneath, Kazuya is:
- Consumed by need for revenge against Iseri
- Ruthless in eliminating obstacles (like Akemi)
- Willing to use anyone (including Fox, his former ally)
- Convinced his cause is just

### The Professional
Despite emotion, Kazuya remains:
- Calculating and patient
- Willing to wait for perfect moment
- Adaptive when plans change
- Skilled at reading people and situations

## Key Scenes to Plan

### Scene 1: First Appearance
When/how does the party first encounter Kazuya?
- Social gathering? (He's established, respectable)
- Suspicious encounter? (They don't know who he is yet)
- Through investigation? (Following Akemi's trail)
- Dramatic reveal? (At a crucial moment)

### Scene 2: Fox's Recognition
Fox recognizes Kazuya (or vice versa):
- Does Fox tell the party immediately?
- Does Kazuya threaten to expose Fox?
- Do they speak privately first?
- What secrets are exchanged?

### Scene 3: Kanji Discovers the Truth
Kanji's investigation leads to Kazuya:
- Direct evidence? Witness testimony? Confession?
- Does Kazuya admit it? Deny it? Justify it?
- Is Fox present? How do they react?
- Can Kanji restrain themselves?

### Scene 4: Iseri's Confrontation
When Iseri learns who Kazuya is:
- Does Kazuya monologue about the brother?
- Does he present "evidence" of the brother's innocence?
- Does he offer Iseri a choice? (Your life for theirs?)
- How does the party respond?

### Scene 5: The Vault Gambit
Kazuya's endgame at the vault:
- Does he arrive first? Follow the party?
- Does he have hostages? Leverage?
- Is he working with Tenka at this point?
- What does he actually want from the vault?

### Scene 6: Final Resolution
Multiple possibilities:
- **Death** - Killed by Iseri, Kanji, Fox, or party
- **Arrest** - Captured and handed to authorities
- **Escape** - Gets away to return another day
- **Redemption?** - Learns truth about his brother? Unlikely but possible
- **Sacrifice** - Dies stopping greater threat (Tenka? Circle?)

## Unanswered Questions

1. **What exactly does Kazuya want from the vault?**
   - Power to kill Iseri perfectly? Artifact? Knowledge? Revenge is the surface motive - what's deeper?

2. **How much does he know about the vault?**
   - Everything? Fragments? Wrong information? 

3. **Is he working with the Obsidian Circle?**
   - Willing member? Unwitting pawn? Using them? Being used?

4. **What is Fox's assassin group called?**
   - And what is its purpose/philosophy?

5. **Why did Kazuya kill Akemi personally?**
   - Could have hired it done. Why risk getting hands dirty?

6. **Does he know about Fox's false memories?**
   - If so, does he know they contain vault information?

7. **What was his relationship with Fox specifically?**
   - Friends? Rivals? Master/student? Equals?

8. **How did his brother really die?**
   - Was Iseri's kill justified? Was the brother corrupt? Or innocent?

9. **What would make Kazuya stop?**
   - Proof brother deserved it? Greater threat? Nothing?

10. **What happens to Fox's group after this?**
    - Will they come looking for Fox or Kazuya?

## Connection to Other NPCs

### Tenka (The Traitor Oniwaban)
- Do they work together? Against each other? Unaware of each other?
- Both want vault access - conflict or cooperation?
- Could party play them against each other?

### Kaoru (Wild Lotus Society)
- Does Kaoru know about Kazuya?
- Is Wild Lotus investigating him?
- Did he manipulate Kaoru's "coincidental" meeting with party?

### The Twice Cursed Samurai
- Is there a connection?
- Did Kazuya disturb/involve the samurai somehow?
- Does the samurai know about Kazuya's crimes?

### Master Hideo's Grandson
- Does Kazuya know about the "blood key"?
- Is the grandson in danger from Kazuya?
- Has Kazuya already approached/manipulated him?

## Character Stats/Build
[To be determined based on party level and desired challenge]

**Suggested Level:** CR 7-9 (matching or slightly exceeding party)

**Archetype:** Assassin Rogue / Shadow Monk multiclass

**Key Abilities:**
- High DEX and INT
- Expertise in Stealth, Deception, Insight
- Poison mastery
- Shadow Step or similar teleportation
- Sneak Attack damage
- Stunning Strike
- Patient Defense

**Signature Moves:**
- **Brother's Vengeance** - Enhanced attack against Iseri specifically
- **Assassin's Mark** - Knows Fox's patterns, advantage against them
- **Shadow Kill** - How he murdered Akemi (leaves no trace)
- **Information Network** - Can call for backup/intelligence in Takashima

## Motivations & Philosophy

**Surface Motivation:** Revenge for brother's death

**Deeper Truth:** [Choose one or combine]
1. **Guilt** - Feels responsible for brother's death (should have protected him)
2. **Identity** - Revenge gives him purpose; without it, he's nothing
3. **Justice** - Genuinely believes Iseri is a criminal who must pay
4. **Power** - Revenge is excuse; really wants vault's contents
5. **Duty** - Promised brother/mother/someone he'd make this right

**Philosophy:**
"Justice without law is just revenge. Revenge without purpose is just murder. I have both law and purpose."

Or perhaps:
"You took someone precious from me. Now I'll take everything precious from you - your friends, your hope, your life. And only then will we be even."

## Potential Redemption Path

If the GM wants to offer possibility of redemption:

**What Would It Take:**
1. **Learn brother was corrupt** - Undeniable proof the kill was justified
2. **Face greater evil** - Something worse than Iseri threatens everything
3. **Fox's testimony** - His former comrade convinces him
4. **Akemi's forgiveness** - If Akemi somehow isn't dead, or ghost/message
5. **Iseri's sacrifice** - Iseri offers to die to save Kazuya from himself

**Would He Accept It:**
Probably not. But the option creates dramatic tension.

**Cost of Redemption:**
- Turn on Circle/Tenka
- Give up revenge
- Help party stop vault crisis
- Face justice for Akemi's murder
- Lose everything he's built in Takashima

---

*Kazuya is designed to be personal antagonist for THREE PCs simultaneously while tying into the larger vault conspiracy. His multi-layered threat makes him a significant Arc 2 villain worthy of the build-up.*

## Tenka

# Tenka - The Last Oniwaban (Traitor)

## Identity & Title

**Name:** Tenka  
**Title:** The Last Oniwaban (alongside Aoshi, though he lost the right to the name)  
**Other Names:** The Traitor, The Fallen Guardian, The One Who Broke

## The Betrayal

### What He Did
Tenka betrayed the Oniwaban clan to the Obsidian Circle, leading to:
- The systematic massacre of the entire clan
- The exposure of clan secrets and techniques
- The loss of the vault's primary guardians
- The death of everyone Aoshi knew and loved

### Why He Survived
Tenka is the only Oniwaban besides Aoshi to survive the massacre because:
- He orchestrated it
- He gave the Circle the information they needed
- He stood aside (or participated) while his brothers and sisters died
- He earned his life through betrayal

## Current Status

### In Takashima
Tenka is currently in Takashima, positioned to:
- Guard the vault for the Circle (or himself)
- Prevent others from accessing it
- Eliminate Aoshi if they appear
- Serve his own mysterious agenda

### Position & Cover
[To be determined - options:]
- **Official Position:** Guard captain? City official? Military officer?
- **Criminal Underground:** Enforcer? Shadow guild master?
- **Circle Agent:** Open member? Deep cover operative?
- **Independent:** Serving only himself, using Circle resources?

## Relationship to Kazuya

Tenka and Kazuya are **counterparts** - parallel villains who serve as mirrors:

**The Duality:**
- Tenka: Traitor to clan/family for [reason]
- Kazuya: Traitor to assassin brotherhood for revenge

**Potential Dynamic:**
1. **Allied** - Work together to access vault
2. **Rivals** - Competing for vault's contents
3. **Uneasy Partners** - Use each other but don't trust
4. **Unaware** - Don't know about each other initially

**The Mirror:**
Both villains force their PC counterparts to confront betrayal:
- Aoshi must face Tenka (clan betrayer)
- Fox must face Kazuya (brotherhood betrayer)
- Both PCs must decide: Kill? Redeem? Understand?

## The Why - Motivation for Betrayal

**Possible Reasons (Choose one or combine):**

### Option 1: Corrupted by Shindarasu
- Exposed to corruption through vault duty
- Shindarasu whispered promises of power
- Memory manipulation made him believe betrayal was right path
- Wasn't fully willing - corrupted over time

**Pros:** Offers redemption possibility, ties to main villain
**Cons:** Reduces personal agency, might feel like excuse

### Option 2: Ambition & Power
- Wanted power/knowledge the clan kept from him
- Believed Oniwaban way was outdated/wrong
- Circle offered what clan wouldn't
- Fully willing and intentional

**Pros:** Clear villain, no moral ambiguity
**Cons:** Less complex, harder to make sympathetic

### Option 3: Survival & Cowardice
- Circle threatened him specifically
- Chose his life over clan's
- Regrets it now but can't undo it
- Living in guilt and self-hatred

**Pros:** Tragic villain, redemption possible
**Cons:** Makes him weaker, less threatening

### Option 4: Philosophical Disagreement
- Believed vault should be used, not guarded
- Thought Oniwaban secrecy was wrong
- Genuinely believes Circle's goals are right
- Willing traitor for "greater good"

**Pros:** Makes him ideological threat
**Cons:** Requires fleshing out Circle philosophy

### Option 5: Protecting Someone/Something
- Circle held leverage over someone he loved
- Had to betray clan to save [person/place/thing]
- Still believes it was right choice
- Would do it again

**Pros:** Complex motivations, moral gray area
**Cons:** Requires developing the leverage

### Option 6: The Visions Made Him Do It
- Shikigo or Oni Babamon spoke to him first
- Visions told him to betray the clan
- Believes he's following divine will
- Aoshi's visions are the same source

**Pros:** Ties to Aoshi's storyline directly
**Cons:** Makes visions purely evil (removes ambiguity)

## Relationship to Aoshi

### Before the Betrayal

**Who Was Tenka to Aoshi?** (Choose one - most painful option)

1. **Aoshi's Mentor/Teacher**
   - Trained Aoshi in Oniwaban ways
   - Father figure or respected elder
   - Betrayal hits hardest: "You taught me everything"

2. **Aoshi's Brother/Sister (Blood or Sworn)**
   - Closest bond in the clan
   - Grew up together, fought together
   - Betrayal hits hardest: "We were family"

3. **Aoshi's Rival**
   - Competed for position/respect
   - Push each other to improve
   - Betrayal hits hardest: "I trusted you to be better"

4. **Aoshi's Best Friend**
   - Shared everything, no secrets
   - Complete trust and loyalty
   - Betrayal hits hardest: "I would have died for you"

5. **Aoshi's Former Love**
   - Romantic relationship (failed or hidden)
   - Complicated by duty and tradition
   - Betrayal hits hardest: "I loved you"

### The Confrontation

When Aoshi and Tenka finally meet:

**Tenka's Perspective:**
- "I did what I had to do"
- "You don't understand the burden"
- "The clan was already dying"
- "I'm not your enemy"
- "Join me, or die like the rest"

**Aoshi's Perspective:**
- "You killed everyone"
- "You betrayed everything we stood for"
- "I trusted you"
- "Give me one reason not to kill you now"
- "The clan dies with us - there's no coming back"

## Combat Capabilities

### Oniwaban Techniques
As a former guardian, Tenka possesses:
- All traditional Oniwaban combat arts
- Oni-enhanced strength/abilities
- Vault-specific knowledge and counters
- Decades of experience

### Advantages Over Aoshi
- **Experience:** Older, more battles fought
- **Knowledge:** Knows Aoshi's training (he may have trained them)
- **Preparation:** Expects Aoshi might come
- **Resources:** Circle backing, equipment, position
- **Ruthlessness:** No moral limits anymore

### Aoshi's Advantages
- **Righteousness:** Fighting for fallen clan
- **Purity:** Untainted by corruption (yet)
- **Hunger:** Needs this more than Tenka does
- **Allies:** Has the party backing them
- **Youth:** Possibly younger, faster (if Tenka is older)

### Signature Abilities

**Oniwaban Secret Arts:**
- **Oni's Wrath:** Enhanced strength from oni heritage
- **Shadow Guardian Form:** Defensive kata from vault duty
- **Ancestral Techniques:** Clan-specific martial arts
- **Vault Walker:** Knows vault layout and traps

**Corrupted/Enhanced by Circle:**
- **Tsurogami's Blessing:** Additional power from the Woven Tyrant
- **Memory Theft:** Can steal techniques by observing them
- **Shadow Step:** Teleportation through shadows
- **Soulsteel Weapon:** May wield corrupted Oniwaban blade

## Connection to the Vault

### Guardian Turned Infiltrator
Tenka knows:
- All vault entrances (hidden and obvious)
- Trap locations and how to disarm them
- What seals protect what chambers
- What treasures/secrets are inside
- How to use the vault's power

### The Obsidian Circle's Key
The Circle needed Tenka because:
- Only Oniwaban could pass certain wards
- Oni blood required to access some areas
- Guardian knowledge irreplaceable
- Traditional guardians would never cooperate

### Current Role
Is Tenka:
- **Still working for Circle:** Actively helping them
- **Playing both sides:** Using Circle while pursuing own goals
- **Trapped:** Can't escape Circle's hold
- **Regretting:** Wants to undo what he's done
- **Independent:** Used Circle, now working alone

## The Shikigo/Oni Babamon Connection

### Are the Visions Related?

**Possibility 1: Same Source, Different Messages**
- Shikigo speaks to both Aoshi and Tenka
- Gave Tenka one path (betrayal)
- Giving Aoshi different path (redemption?)
- Testing both to see who succeeds

**Possibility 2: Opposing Forces**
- Shikigo guides Aoshi (true guardian spirit)
- Oni Babamon corrupted Tenka (false/evil entity)
- The vision conflict represents larger cosmic struggle
- Aoshi must reject false visions to succeed

**Possibility 3: Tenka Never Had Visions**
- He's lying about divine guidance
- Using it as justification/excuse
- Trying to confuse/corrupt Aoshi
- The visions are unique to Aoshi

**Possibility 4: Corruption of True Visions**
- Tenka received real visions but misinterpreted them
- Circle's corruption twisted his understanding
- Shindarasu interfered with the message
- Aoshi risks the same corruption

### Questions for Aoshi

When Tenka claims to have received the same visions:
- Does Aoshi believe him?
- Does this make Aoshi question their own visions?
- Are the visions trustworthy at all?
- What if Tenka is right?

## Key Scenes to Plan

### Scene 1: The Revelation
How does Aoshi learn Tenka is alive and in Takashima?
- Evidence in investigation?
- Direct encounter?
- Someone tells them?
- Recognizes Oniwaban technique in combat?

### Scene 2: First Confrontation
Their first meeting face-to-face:
- Combat immediately or talk first?
- Private or with witnesses?
- Does Tenka explain himself?
- Can others stop them from killing each other?

### Scene 3: The Truth Unveiled
Tenka's reasons are revealed:
- Full confession?
- Pieced together evidence?
- Deathbed revelation?
- Never fully explained?

### Scene 4: At the Vault
When both Oniwaban are at the vault:
- Do they work together against greater threat?
- Fight each other while crisis unfolds?
- Forced to use their connection to save others?
- Guardian techniques required - only they know them?

### Scene 5: Final Resolution
How does Tenka's story end?
- **Death by Aoshi:** Justice for the clan
- **Death by Sacrifice:** Redeems himself saving others
- **Captured:** Lives but imprisoned/exiled
- **Redeemed:** Joins party/becomes ally
- **Escapes:** Survives for future arc
- **Consumed by Corruption:** Becomes monster, must be put down

### Scene 6: Aoshi's Choice
The moment that defines Aoshi:
- Kill Tenka in revenge?
- Spare him and risk more betrayal?
- Understand his reasons?
- Forgive the unforgivable?
- Continue the cycle or break it?

## Personality & Behavior

### Surface Presentation
How Tenka appears to others in Takashima:
- [Dignified? Haunted? Cold? Efficient?]
- [Respected? Feared? Pitied? Unknown?]
- [Obvious villain or hidden in plain sight?]

### With Aoshi
When confronting the last of his clan:
- Remorseful? Defiant? Nostalgic? Angry?
- Does he want Aoshi's forgiveness?
- Does he want Aoshi to join him?
- Does he want Aoshi to kill him?
- Does he want Aoshi to understand?

### True Self
Beneath all masks:
- Does he regret what he did?
- Does he believe it was necessary?
- Is he consumed by guilt or free of it?
- Has corruption changed who he was?
- Is there anything left of the guardian he was?

## Unanswered Questions

1. **What is Tenka's exact relationship to Aoshi?**
   - Choose the most painful option for maximum impact

2. **Why did he betray the clan?**
   - This answer defines whether redemption is possible

3. **Where was Aoshi when the massacre happened?**
   - Does Tenka know Aoshi survived? Did he ensure it? Try to prevent it?

4. **What does Tenka want from the vault now?**
   - Power? Redemption? Destruction? Nothing - just guarding it?

5. **Is he working with Kazuya?**
   - Allied, rivals, or unaware of each other?

6. **Did he have a choice?**
   - Was betrayal truly the only option, or does he tell himself that?

7. **Does he know about Aoshi's visions?**
   - If so, what does he make of them?

8. **Can he still use full Oniwaban techniques?**
   - Or has corruption/betrayal cost him some abilities?

9. **What happened to the other Oniwaban's bodies?**
   - Proper burial? Left to rot? Desecrated? Does Tenka know?

10. **What would make him switch sides?**
    - Threat to something he still cares about? Proof Circle lied? Nothing?

## Integration with Other Storylines

### Fox's False Memories
Do Fox's implanted memories contain information about:
- The Oniwaban massacre?
- Tenka's betrayal?
- Vault defenses the Oniwaban protected?
- How to contact/find Tenka?

### Yasei's Hikari
Is Tenka aware that:
- Hikari is fading?
- The Oniwaban protected Hikari's portal?
- His betrayal contributed to Hikari's weakening?
- Deep Village suffered because of him?

### Thornbringer's Quest
Does Tenka know:
- The Well's true nature and location?
- The One Tree's connection to vault?
- How to save or destroy them?
- Fox-kami's relationship to the vault?

### Kanji's Investigation
Does Tenka's presence relate to:
- Akemi's death? (Did Akemi investigate Oniwaban massacre?)
- Kazuya's plans?
- The tea house haunting?

### Iseri's Story
Is there any connection between:
- Tenka and Kazuya's alliance?
- Oniwaban and the assassin brotherhood?
- Guardian duty and vigilante justice?

## Potential Redemption Arc

**If redemption is possible, what would it require?**

**Stage 1: Recognition**
- Tenka must acknowledge what he did was wrong
- Not just for survival or pragmatism - actually wrong
- Face the weight of all those deaths

**Stage 2: Truth**
- Reveal his true motivations honestly
- No more lies, no more justifications
- Accept Aoshi's (and party's) judgment

**Stage 3: Atonement**
- Active steps to undo damage
- Help stop Circle's vault plan
- Restore Oniwaban honor somehow
- Probably requires sacrifice

**Stage 4: Acceptance**
- Even if redeemed, can never be Oniwaban again
- Must live with what he did
- Aoshi may never forgive him
- Some sins leave permanent scars

**Stage 5: New Purpose**
- If he survives, what does he do?
- Become new guardian (elsewhere)?
- Serve Aoshi's mission?
- Wandering ronin seeking redemption?
- Death may be the only true atonement

## Character Build/Stats
[To be determined based on party level]

**Suggested Level:** CR 8-10 (elite difficulty for Aoshi's personal boss)

**Archetype:** Way of Shadow Monk / Fighter (Battle Master or Samurai)

**Key Abilities:**
- High DEX, WIS, CON
- Expertise in Stealth, Perception, Athletics
- Oni racial traits (advantage on STR checks, darkvision, etc.)
- Shadow Step, Pass Without Trace
- Action Surge, Extra Attack (2-3)
- Martial Arts die scaling
- Stunning Strike
- Defensive abilities (Patient Defense, Deflect Missiles)

**Signature Moves:**
- **Oniwaban Secret Technique: [Name]** - Clan-specific ability
- **Traitor's Advantage** - Knows how Aoshi fights
- **Vault Guardian's Ward** - Defensive technique from protection duty
- **Oni's Heritage: [Name]** - Racial power enhanced by training
- **Shadow of Betrayal** - Corruption-enhanced ability

**Legendary Actions (if appropriate):**
- Move without provoking opportunity attacks
- Stunning Strike additional target
- Defensive stance granting resistance

## Philosophy & Dialogue

**Sample Quotes:**

*On the Betrayal:*
"You think I wanted this? I carried that burden every day. But someone had to make the hard choice."

*On the Visions:*
"Shikigo showed me the same path it's showing you now. Do you have the strength to see it through?"

*On Aoshi Surviving:*
"I made sure you weren't there that night. If nothing else, let that mean something."

*On Redemption:*
"There is no redemption for what I've done. Only consequences. And I've been running from them for too long."

*On the Clan:*
"The Oniwaban were already dead. I just accepted it before you did."

*On the Vault:*
"We swore to guard it with our lives. I chose to guard it with their lives instead. That's the difference between us."

---

*Tenka is designed as Aoshi's ultimate test - the living embodiment of betrayal, the last connection to the fallen clan, and the choice between vengeance and understanding. His resolution will define what kind of Oniwaban Aoshi chooses to be.*

## Twice Cursed Samurai

# Twice Cursed Samurai

## Overview
A mysterious figure in samurai armor haunting an old tea house in Takashima. Reported as a vengeful spirit by locals, but the truth is far more complex.

## Current Situation
**Location:** Old tea house in Takashima  
**Status:** Mistaken for a vengeful spirit  
**Reputation:** Feared by locals

### Reported Manifestations
- Figure appearing in full samurai armor
- Objects moving on their own
- Sudden temperature drops
- Injuries to visitors (though no deaths)
- Recent onset - something triggered the haunting

## The Truth

The Twice Cursed Samurai is **not evil** and **did not murder Master Hideo**.

**Key Facts:**
- Not actually a vengeful spirit (or not only that)
- Innocent of Master Hideo's death
- Reasons for haunting the tea house unknown
- True nature and purpose unclear

## Master Hideo Connection

Master Hideo's murder has been attributed to the Twice Cursed Samurai, but this is false. The real circumstances of Hideo's death remain a mystery.

**Questions:**
- What actually happened to Master Hideo?
- Why is the Twice Cursed Samurai being blamed?
- Who benefits from this false accusation?
- Does the Twice Cursed Samurai know the truth about Hideo's death?

## The "Twice Cursed" Title

The name suggests dual curses or afflictions. 

**Possible interpretations:**
- Cursed in life and death
- Two separate curses affecting him
- Cursed by two different sources
- Metaphorical - cursed by fate twice over

## Abilities

[To be discovered during investigation]
- Can manifest physically
- Moves objects (telekinesis or physical manipulation?)
- Affects temperature (spiritual presence or deliberate power?)
- Can injure but hasn't killed
- Appears in full armor (always, or by choice?)

## Mysteries

1. **Identity:** Who was he in life?
2. **The Curses:** What are the two curses?
3. **The Tea House:** Why this location specifically?
4. **Master Hideo:** What's the real story?
5. **Intent:** What does he want?
6. **Communication:** Can he speak? Will he?
7. **Resolution:** What would allow him to rest (if that's even what he wants)?

## Investigation Hooks

- Interview witnesses about what they've seen
- Examine the tea house for clues
- Research Master Hideo's death
- Attempt communication with the spirit
- Look for connections between Hideo and the samurai
- Investigate who spread the murder accusation
- Check historical records about the tea house

## Wild Lotus Society Intel

The [[Wild Lotus Society]] (through [[Kaoru Sugimoto]]) knows:
- Multiple credible witness reports
- Pattern of manifestations
- No fatalities despite injuries
- Recent onset of activity

What they apparently don't know (or aren't sharing):
- The samurai's true nature
- His innocence regarding Master Hideo
- What triggered his appearance

## Campaign Implications

This investigation may reveal:
- Corruption or conspiracy around Master Hideo's death
- Larger supernatural events in Takashima
- The true purpose of the Wild Lotus Society
- Connections to Arc 2's main plot

## Nagano

# Nagano

Nagano sits at the edge of the Frostwood within [Takashima Prefecture](Takashima%20Prefecture.md). It is a modest forest village that balances reverence for [Master Hideo Nakata](Master%20Hideo%20Nakata.md)'s legacy with the practical needs of travelers, hunters, and shrine-keepers. The settlement acts as the party's launch point for *The Last Whisper of Steel*, providing resources, rumors, and a steady pulse of local drama.

## Services for Adventurers

### Rewards & Contacts
- **Mayor Hiroshi Takeda:** Assigns civic contracts and pays bounties through his steward at Takeda Manor.
- **The Path of Blades:** A small adventurers' hall run by **Kana Yoshiro**, a retired swordswoman who tracks quests and mediates disputes.
- **Temple of the Three Moons:** **Reiko Matsuda** grants blessings, relics, or divine favors for services rendered to the kami.

### Rest Options
- **The Jade Lantern Inn:** Mid-tier rooms, warm baths, and gossip courtesy of proprietors **Yuto and Aiko Mori**.
- **Temple Sanctuary:** Communal mats for pilgrims and those who aid the shrine.
- **Village Hospitality:** Locals such as [Aya the Weaver](Aya%20the%20Weaver.md) or [Taro the Hunter](Taro%20the%20Hunter.md) occasionally host travelers in exchange for stories or favors.
- **Frostwood Camps:** Militia-protected campsite just outside the village walls for those preferring the open air.

### Resupply & Craftsmanship
- **Market District:** Everyday gear, provisions, and minor talismans traded beneath silk awnings.
- **Haruto's Forge:** Crafts functional blades and repairs Soulsteel heirlooms with reverence.
- **Ayame's Apothecary:** Stocks healing salves, herbal poultices, and rare Frostwood botanicals.
- **Temple Blessings:** Priests inscribe protective charms or sanctify equipment prior to expeditions.

### Research & Lore
- **Temple Library:** Scrolls on regional kami, yokai sightings, and ceremonial calendars; Reiko serves as guide.
- **Storyteller's Hearth:** Alehouse where **Renji "Driftwood" Sato** gathers rumors from smugglers and hunters.
- **Village Elders:** **Yumi Harada** and other retirees share oral histories and guidance on surviving the Frostwood.

### Recreation & Downtime
- **Spring Equinox Festival:** Seasonal games, dances, and offerings to the Earth Kami; ideal for roleplay interludes.
- **Frostwood Dojo:** Sparring lessons with [Riku Matsunaga](Riku%20Matsunaga.md) for those wanting a training montage.
- **Jade Lantern Games Night:** Dice, cards, and storytelling contests--wagers often pay in favors, not coin.
- **Artisan Workshops:** Rotating demonstrations in weaving, pottery, and smithing; participants craft keepsakes or gifts.

## Key NPCs
- **Hiroshi Takeda:** Pragmatic mayor safeguarding Nagano's autonomy.
- **Kana Yoshiro:** Guildmaster who keeps tabs on factions moving through the region.
- **Reiko Matsuda:** Head priest balancing kami diplomacy with political pressure.
- **Haruto the Blacksmith:** Devoted apprentice of Hideo, anxious to uphold his mentor's standards.
- **Renji "Driftwood" Sato:** Informant and conflicted member of [The Grey Tide](The%20Grey%20Tide.md).

## Unique Features
- **Whispering Glade:** Resting place of Master Hideo; the funeral scene and many visions originate here.
- **Frostwood Outpost:** Frontier watch post managed by Nagano's militia; doubles as quest hub for wilderness threats.
- **Weaver's Market:** A decadal market bringing artisans from surrounding villages and traveling merchants under a single canopy.

## Quest Board Highlights

| Quest | Patron | Summary | Reward |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **"Find My Cat"** | Aya the Weaver | Track down mischievous Tama near the Frostwood edge before yokai notice. | Insight charm (+1 Insight for 24 hours). |
| **Herbal Gathering** | Ayame the Apothecary | Harvest Moonshade Blossoms guarded by restless spirits. | Healing potion and future discount. |
| **Repair the River Shrine** | Reiko Matsuda | Restore a vandalized shrine to appease water kami. | Temporary blessing (+1 Dexterity checks for a day). |
| **Missing Hunter** | Taro the Hunter | Locate Ichika after she vanishes during a Frostwood patrol. | Fine hunting dagger. |
| **Odd Noises at Night** | Yumi Harada | Investigate eerie sounds haunting a retired hunter's homestead. | Secret footpath into the Frostwood. |
| **The Stolen Relic** | Reiko Matsuda | Recover a temple relic pilfered from the Market District. | Minor enchanted pendant (casts *Light* once per day). |
| **Merchant's Caravan in Peril** | Mayor Hiroshi Takeda | Rescue a delayed caravan near the Frostwood trail. | 25 gp and mayoral goodwill. |
| **Yokai Infestation** | Frostwood Militia | Disperse kappa harassing village wells. | 15 gp bounty and militia favor. |

## Market Faces
- **Hana Yamaguchi:** Master barterer; general goods and trail supplies.
- **Kenta "Two Coins":** Vendor of charms and dubious relics--great for plot hooks.
- **Akiko Tanaka:** Fine textiles co-created with Aya; outfits dignitaries and performers.
- **Isamu Takahashi:** Grizzled armourer producing reliable kits for guards and travelers.
- **Mariko Fujimoto:** Sells fresh produce, herbs, and festival delicacies.

Traveling caravans rotate through the Weaver's Market every ten days, bringing lumber from Hagane, preserved mountain fare from Futaba, Frostiron ingots from Shirogane, and luxurious silks from Shimizu. Treat the market as a living backdrop--prices shift with political tension, and the right merchant always seems to know a secret worth chasing.

## Wild Lotus Society

# Wild Lotus Society

## Overview
A group of ghost hunters and demon slayers who work on behalf of the citizenry, operating outside official channels. The Wild Lotus Society protects common people from supernatural threats that government officials often ignore or can't handle.

## Purpose & Methods
- Hunt ghosts, demons, and other supernatural threats
- Work on behalf of ordinary citizens
- Operate independently of official government structures
- Mix investigation, combat, and spiritual expertise
- Accept payment but prioritize protecting people over profit

## Known Members

### [[Kaoru Sugimoto]]
The elegant merchant and gambler revealed to be a member during the party's arrival in Takashima. Her supernatural abilities (demonstrated in the coin-splitting trick) now make more sense in context - she's a trained warrior and spirit hunter, not just a skilled performer.

**Role:** Field agent, investigator
**Skills:** Combat (possibly wind-based techniques), investigation, social manipulation
**Cover:** Travels as a merchant, uses gambling to gather information and make connections

## Operations

The Society appears to use covers and mundane occupations to move freely while investigating supernatural threats. Members maintain public personas while secretly hunting ghosts and demons.

### Known Tactics
- Maintain civilian covers (merchants, gamblers, etc.)
- Gather intelligence through social networks
- Recruit capable outsiders for specific missions
- Pay for services when needed
- Investigate before engaging

## Current Activities in Takashima

The Society has identified a haunting at an old tea house. Rather than handle it directly, [[Kaoru Sugimoto]] recruited the party (as payment for getting them into the city) to investigate and deal with the spirit.

**The Tea House Assignment:**
- Location: Old tea house in Takashima
- Threat: Supposed vengeful spirit (actually the [[Twice Cursed Samurai]])
- Reported activity: Figure in samurai armor, moving objects, temperature drops
- Status: Under investigation by the party

## Questions & Mysteries

- How large is the Wild Lotus Society?
- Who leads it?
- How do they choose their members?
- What's their relationship with official authorities?
- Do they only operate in Takashima, or throughout the region?
- What supernatural threats have they faced before?
- Why use the party instead of handling the tea house themselves?
- What other members might the party encounter?

## Organizational Structure
[To be determined through play]

## Resources & Capabilities
- Members with supernatural abilities
- Network of information gatherers
- Ability to operate across regions (Kaoru travels the Silver Road)
- Funds to hire external help when needed
- Knowledge of spirits, demons, and how to combat them

## Campaign Notes
- Revealed to party: Arc 2, at the gates of Takashima
- First mission assigned: Clear the haunted tea house
- Kaoru's membership explains her mysterious abilities

## Iseri Tetsuo

# Iseri Tetsuo

## Player Information
**Player:** Dane  
**Introduced:** Arc 2, Road to Takashima

## Background

### Early Life
Iseri Tetsuo grew up in a modest carpenter's home, keenly aware of the injustice surrounding him. His parents never spoke openly about their struggles, but young Tetsuo saw how heavy-handed taxes and underpaying commissions from their leaders kept honest craftspeople from ever getting ahead.

What started as childhood pranks on tax collectors and corrupt officials became something more. Local business owners, grateful for the small rebellions, would reward him with trinkets and snacks. This validation planted a seed - the understanding that he could make a difference, however small, against the system that crushed people like his parents.

### The Descent into Shadow
As Tetsuo grew older, pranks gave way to training. He developed martial skills with a singular purpose: to make a bigger impact against corruption and exploitation. The boy who once loosened wagon wheels evolved into a young man who could do real damage to those he saw as oppressors.

At 19, everything changed. Tetsuo lured a drunken collections officer into the wilderness for what he intended as another lesson. The confrontation turned physical. It ended with Tetsuo standing over his first kill.

### Life in the Shadows
After that night, there was no going back. Tetsuo retreated deeper into the shadows, working from darkness to avoid capture. His crusade against corrupt officials continued, but now the stakes were life and death - both for his targets and for himself.

## Appearance
[To be determined in play]

## Personality Traits
- Strong sense of justice (albeit a vigilante version)
- Willing to use violence to achieve his goals
- Operates from the shadows
- Resourceful and tactical
- Carries the weight of his first kill

## Current Situation
When the party encounters Tetsuo on the road to Takashima, he is unconscious and restrained by a six-armed troll. Four dead bodies litter the ground around them - the aftermath of whatever confrontation led to his capture. The circumstances that brought him to this point remain unclear.

## Questions & Mysteries
- What was Tetsuo doing on the road to Takashima?
- Who are the four dead bodies around the troll?
- Was Tetsuo hunting someone, or was he being hunted?
- How will he react to being rescued by strangers?
- Will his vigilante methods clash with the party's approach?

## Campaign Notes

- Rescued by the party (or at least found by them)
- Entry into Takashima complicated by his unknown status
